import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpInterceptorFn } from '@angular/common/http';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { catchError, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

export const authInterceptor: HttpInterceptorFn = (req, next) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);
  const token = authService.getToken();

  let authReq = req;
  if (token && !authService.isTokenExpired(token)) {
    authReq = req.clone({
      setHeaders: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    });
  }

  return next(authReq).pipe(
    catchError((error: HttpErrorResponse) => {
      if (error.status === 401) {
        authService.logout();
        try {
          router.navigate(['/login'])?.catch?.(() => {});
        } catch {
          // ignore navigation errors in unit test environments without configured routes
        }
      }
      return throwError(() => error);
    })
  );
};

import { Injectable, InjectionToken, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, tap } from 'rxjs';

export interface UserContext {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  roles: string[];
  is_superadmin: boolean;
  permissions?: string[];
}

export interface AuthResponse {
  access_token: string;
  token_type: string;
  user?: UserContext;
}

export interface LoginRequest {
  username_or_email: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface JwtPayload {
  sub?: string;
  username?: string;
  email?: string;
  roles?: string[];
  is_superadmin?: boolean;
  permissions?: string[];
  exp?: number;
  iat?: number;
  nbf?: number;
  jti?: string;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

export const AUTH_STORAGE_KEY = 'datacatalog_access_token';

/**
 * Injection token for storage abstraction.
 * Falls back to an in-memory Storage implementation in environments where
 * window.localStorage is not accessible (e.g. Node 22+ runners, SSR).
 */
export const AUTH_STORAGE = new InjectionToken<Storage>('AUTH_STORAGE', {
  providedIn: 'root',
  factory: () => {
    try {
      if (
        typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
        window.localStorage &&
        typeof window.localStorage.getItem === 'function'
      ) {
        return window.localStorage;
      }
    } catch {
      // Fall through to in-memory store
    }

    const store = new Map<string, string>();
    return {
      getItem: (key: string): string | null => store.get(key) ?? null,
      setItem: (key: string, value: string): void => {
        store.set(key, String(value));
      },
      removeItem: (key: string): void => {
        store.delete(key);
      },
      clear: (): void => {
        store.clear();
      },
      key: (index: number): string | null => Array.from(store.keys())[index] ?? null,
      get length(): number {
        return store.size;
      },
    } as Storage;
  },
});

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class AuthService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient, { optional: true });
  private readonly storage = inject(AUTH_STORAGE);

  private readonly _token = signal<string | null>(null);
  private readonly _currentUser = signal<UserContext | null>(null);

  public readonly token = this._token.asReadonly();
  public readonly currentUser = this._currentUser.asReadonly();

  public readonly isAuthenticated = computed(() => {
    const currentToken = this._token();
    return !!currentToken && !this.isTokenExpired(currentToken);
  });

  constructor() {
    this.restoreSessionFromStorage();
  }

  public getToken(): string | null {
    return this._token();
  }

  public setToken(token: string): void {
    if (!token) {
      this.logout();
      return;
    }

    const payload = this.decodePayload(token);
    if (!payload) {
      return;
    }

    const user: UserContext = {
      id: payload.sub ?? '',
      username: payload.username ?? '',
      email: payload.email ?? '',
      roles: Array.isArray(payload.roles) ? [...payload.roles] : [],
      is_superadmin: Boolean(payload.is_superadmin),
      permissions: Array.isArray(payload.permissions) ? [...payload.permissions] : [],
    };

    this._token.set(token);
    this._currentUser.set(user);
    this.storage.setItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY, token);
  }

  public logout(): void {
    this._token.set(null);
    this._currentUser.set(null);
    this.storage.removeItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
  }

  public login(credentials: LoginRequest): Observable<AuthResponse> {
    if (!this.http) {
      throw new Error('HttpClient is not available in the current injection context');
    }

    return this.http.post<AuthResponse>('/api/v1/auth/login', credentials).pipe(
      tap((response: AuthResponse) => {
        if (response?.access_token) {
          this.setToken(response.access_token);
        }
      })
    );
  }

  public isTokenExpired(token?: string | null): boolean {
    const targetToken = token !== undefined ? token : this._token();
    if (!targetToken) {
      return true;
    }

    const payload = this.decodePayload(targetToken);
    if (!payload || typeof payload.exp !== 'number') {
      return true;
    }

    const currentEpochSeconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    return payload.exp <= currentEpochSeconds;
  }

  public decodePayload(token: string): JwtPayload | null {
    try {
      const parts = token.split('.');
      if (parts.length !== 3) {
        return null;
      }

      let base64 = parts[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
      while (base64.length % 4 !== 0) {
        base64 += '=';
      }

      const binaryString = atob(base64);
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(binaryString.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < binaryString.length; i++) {
        bytes[i] = binaryString.charCodeAt(i);
      }

      const decodedText = new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(bytes);
      return JSON.parse(decodedText) as JwtPayload;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private restoreSessionFromStorage(): void {
    const storedToken = this.storage.getItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!storedToken) {
      return;
    }

    if (!this.isTokenExpired(storedToken)) {
      this.setToken(storedToken);
    } else {
      this.storage.removeItem(AUTH_STORAGE_KEY);
    }
  }
}

export const ROLE_PERMISSIONS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  SUPERADMIN: ALL_PERMISSION_ACTIONS,
  ADMIN: ALL_PERMISSION_ACTIONS,
  DATA_STEWARD: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EDIT_DESCRIPTION,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_TAGS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATASTEWARD: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EDIT_DESCRIPTION,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_TAGS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATA_ENGINEER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_CONNECTIONS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATAENGINEER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.MANAGE_CONNECTIONS,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  ANALYST: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATAANALYST: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  DATA_ANALYST: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
    PermissionAction.EXPORT_REPORTS,
  ],
  VIEWER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
  ],
  CATALOG_VIEWER: [
    PermissionAction.VIEW_METADATA,
  ],
};

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class RbacService {
  private readonly authService = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly _customPermissions = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  public readonly customPermissions = this._customPermissions.asReadonly();

  /**
   * Computed reactive signal containing all effective uppercase permission strings
   * resolved from superadmin status, explicit user permissions, assigned roles,
   * and custom overrides.
   */
  public readonly effectivePermissions = computed<Set<string>>(() => {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return new Set();
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return new Set(ALL_PERMISSION_ACTIONS.map(action => action.toUpperCase()));
    }

    const resolved = new Set<string>();

    // Add permissions from user context (e.g. from token)
    if (Array.isArray(user.permissions)) {
      for (const perm of user.permissions) {
        if (typeof perm === 'string' && perm.trim()) {
          resolved.add(perm.trim().toUpperCase());
        }
      }
    }

    // Add permissions mapped from user roles
    if (Array.isArray(user.roles)) {
      for (const role of user.roles) {
        const normalizedRole = role?.trim().toUpperCase() ?? '';
        const rolePerms = ROLE_PERMISSIONS[normalizedRole];
        if (Array.isArray(rolePerms)) {
          for (const perm of rolePerms) {
            resolved.add(perm.toUpperCase());
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Add dynamic custom permissions
    for (const perm of this._customPermissions()) {
      resolved.add(perm.toUpperCase());
    }

    return resolved;
  });

  /**
   * Checks whether the current user has the required permission (or all permissions if an array is provided).
   * Reactive when invoked inside an Angular effect() or computed().
   */
  public hasPermission(action: string | string[]): boolean {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return false;
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return true;
    }

    const permissions = this.effectivePermissions();

    if (Array.isArray(action)) {
      if (action.length === 0) {
        return true;
      }
      return action.every(a => typeof a === 'string' && permissions.has(a.trim().toUpperCase()));
    }

    if (typeof action !== 'string' || !action.trim()) {
      return false;
    }

    return permissions.has(action.trim().toUpperCase());
  }

  /**
   * Checks whether the current user has ANY of the specified permissions.
   */
  public hasAnyPermission(actions: string[]): boolean {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return false;
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return true;
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(actions) || actions.length === 0) {
      return false;
    }

    const permissions = this.effectivePermissions();
    return actions.some(a => typeof a === 'string' && permissions.has(a.trim().toUpperCase()));
  }

  /**
   * Checks whether the current user has a specific role or any role from the provided list.
   */
  public hasRole(role: string | string[]): boolean {
    const user = this.authService.currentUser();
    if (!user) {
      return false;
    }

    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      return true;
    }

    const userRoles = new Set((user.roles ?? []).map(r => r.toUpperCase()));

    if (Array.isArray(role)) {
      return role.some(r => userRoles.has(r.trim().toUpperCase()));
    }

    return userRoles.has(role.trim().toUpperCase());
  }

  /**
   * Sets custom dynamic permissions overriding or augmenting role-based permissions.
   */
  public setPermissions(permissions: string[]): void {
    const normalized = new Set(
      permissions
        .filter(p => typeof p === 'string' && p.trim().length > 0)
        .map(p => p.trim().toUpperCase())
    );
    this._customPermissions.set(normalized);
  }

  /**
   * Grants a single permission dynamically.
   */
  public grantPermission(permission: string): void {
    if (!permission || typeof permission !== 'string') {
      return;
    }
    const current = new Set(this._customPermissions());
    current.add(permission.trim().toUpperCase());
    this._customPermissions.set(current);
  }

  /**
   * Revokes a single dynamic permission.
   */
  public revokePermission(permission: string): void {
    if (!permission || typeof permission !== 'string') {
      return;
    }
    const current = new Set(this._customPermissions());
    current.delete(permission.trim().toUpperCase());
    this._customPermissions.set(current);
  }

  /**
   * Clears all custom permissions.
   */
  public clearPermissions(): void {
    this._customPermissions.set(new Set());
  }
}


=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\core\directives\has-permission.directive.ts ===
import {
  Directive,
  Input,
  TemplateRef,
  ViewContainerRef,
  effect,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { RbacService } from '../auth/rbac.service';

/**
 * Structural directive conditionally rendering DOM elements based on RBAC permissions.
 *
 * Driven reactively by Angular Signals:
 * Re-evaluates automatically when user authentication, role assignment, or input parameters change.
 *
 * Usage:
 * ```html
 * <button *hasPermission="'EDIT_DESCRIPTION'">Edit Table</button>
 *
 * <div *hasPermission="['VIEW_METADATA', 'MANAGE_TAGS']">Governance Controls</div>
 *
 * <div *hasPermission="'VIEW_AUDIT_LOGS'; else accessDenied">Audit Log</div>
 * <ng-template #accessDenied><span>Access Restricted</span></ng-template>
 * ```
 */
@Directive({
  selector: '[hasPermission]',
  standalone: true,
})
export class HasPermissionDirective {
  private readonly rbacService = inject(RbacService);
  private readonly templateRef = inject(TemplateRef<unknown>);
  private readonly viewContainer = inject(ViewContainerRef);

  private readonly _action = signal<string | string[]>('');
  private readonly _elseTemplate = signal<TemplateRef<unknown> | null>(null);

  private currentView: 'main' | 'else' | 'none' = 'none';

  @Input('hasPermission')
  set hasPermission(action: string | string[] | null | undefined) {
    this._action.set(action ?? '');
  }

  @Input('hasPermissionElse')
  set hasPermissionElse(template: TemplateRef<unknown> | null | undefined) {
    this._elseTemplate.set(template ?? null);
  }

  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      const action = this._action();
      const elseTpl = this._elseTemplate();

      // Read effectivePermissions to establish reactive dependency on RBAC state
      const allowed = this.rbacService.hasPermission(action);

      if (allowed) {
        if (this.currentView !== 'main') {
          this.viewContainer.clear();
          this.viewContainer.createEmbeddedView(this.templateRef);
          this.currentView = 'main';
        }
      } else if (elseTpl) {
        if (this.currentView !== 'else') {
          this.viewContainer.clear();
          this.viewContainer.createEmbeddedView(elseTpl);
          this.currentView = 'else';
        }
      } else {
        if (this.currentView !== 'none') {
          this.viewContainer.clear();
          this.currentView = 'none';
        }
      }
    });
  }
}

import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../auth/auth.service';

export const authGuard: CanActivateFn = (_route, _state) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  if (authService.isAuthenticated()) {
    return true;
  }

  return router.parseUrl('/login');
};

import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
  CanActivateFn,
  Router,
  RouterStateSnapshot,
  UrlTree,
} from '@angular/router';
import { RbacService } from '../auth/rbac.service';

/**
 * Functional permission route guard enforcing RBAC authorization.
 *
 * Supports two usage patterns:
 * 1. Factory invocation in route config:
 *    `canActivate: [permissionGuard('EDIT_DESCRIPTION')]`
 *    `canActivate: [permissionGuard(['VIEW_METADATA', 'MANAGE_TAGS'])]`
 *
 * 2. Direct CanActivateFn registration relying on route data:
 *    `canActivate: [permissionGuard]`
 *    `data: { permission: 'EDIT_DESCRIPTION' }`
 */
export function permissionGuard(requiredAction: string | string[]): CanActivateFn;
export function permissionGuard(
  route: ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
  state: RouterStateSnapshot
): boolean | UrlTree;
export function permissionGuard(
  arg1: string | string[] | ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
  arg2?: RouterStateSnapshot
): CanActivateFn | boolean | UrlTree {
  if (typeof arg1 === 'string' || Array.isArray(arg1)) {
    const requiredAction = arg1;
    return (
      _route: ActivatedRouteSnapshot,
      _state: RouterStateSnapshot
    ): boolean | UrlTree => {
      const rbacService = inject(RbacService);
      const router = inject(Router);

      if (rbacService.hasPermission(requiredAction)) {
        return true;
      }
      return router.parseUrl('/unauthorized');
    };
  }

  // Invoked directly by the Angular Router as CanActivateFn
  const route = arg1 as ActivatedRouteSnapshot;
  const rbacService = inject(RbacService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  const requiredAction = (route.data?.['permission'] ??
    route.data?.['action'] ??
    route.data?.['requiredAction']) as string | string[] | undefined;

  if (!requiredAction || rbacService.hasPermission(requiredAction)) {
    return true;
  }

  return router.parseUrl('/unauthorized');
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { RoleManagementService } from '../services/role-management.service';
import {
  PermissionDefinition,
  PermissionModule,
  RoleCreateDto,
  RoleItem,
  RoleUpdateDto,
  RoleUserItem,
} from '../models/access-control.models';

export interface FlatPermissionItem {
  moduleKey: string;
  moduleName: string;
  action: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-role-management',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterModule],
  templateUrl: './role-management.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./role-management.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class RoleManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  private roleService = inject(RoleManagementService);

  // Roles list state
  rawRoles = signal<RoleItem[]>([]);
  currentPage = signal<number>(1);
  pageSize = signal<number>(5);
  availablePageSizes = [5, 10, 20, 50];

  // Column search filters & Unified search
  searchTerm = signal<string>('');
  filterPersianName = signal<string>('');
  filterEnglishName = signal<string>('');

  isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  isSaving = signal<boolean>(false);
  errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  successMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Master-Detail selection & Form state
  selectedRole = signal<RoleItem | null>(null);
  isCreateMode = signal<boolean>(false);
  isEditMode = signal<boolean>(false);
  isFormEditable = computed<boolean>(() => this.isCreateMode() || this.isEditMode());

  // Form Fields (English system name & Persian display title)
  formEnglishName = signal<string>('');
  formPersianName = signal<string>('');

  // Sub-tabs in detail panel: 'permissions' | 'users'
  activeSubTab = signal<'permissions' | 'users'>('permissions');

  // Permissions registry & current role permissions
  permissionModules = signal<PermissionModule[]>([]);
  roleSelectedActions = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());
  permissionFilterQuery = signal<string>('');

  // Users assigned to selected role
  roleUsers = signal<RoleUserItem[]>([]);
  isLoadingRoleUsers = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Filtered and Paginated View Models
  filteredRoles = computed<RoleItem[]>(() => {
    const list = this.rawRoles();
    const search = this.searchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    const faFilter = this.filterPersianName().trim().toLowerCase();
    const enFilter = this.filterEnglishName().trim().toLowerCase();

    return list.filter((r) => {
      const pName = (r.description || r.name).toLowerCase();
      const eName = r.name.toLowerCase();
      if (search && !pName.includes(search) && !eName.includes(search)) return false;
      if (faFilter && !pName.includes(faFilter)) return false;
      if (enFilter && !eName.includes(enFilter)) return false;
      return true;
    });
  });

  paginatedRoles = computed<RoleItem[]>(() => {
    const all = this.filteredRoles();
    const start = (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize();
    return all.slice(start, start + this.pageSize());
  });

  totalPages = computed<number>(() => {
    const count = this.filteredRoles().length;
    return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(count / this.pageSize()));
  });

  pageDisplayStart = computed<number>(() => {
    if (this.filteredRoles().length === 0) return 0;
    return (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize() + 1;
  });

  pageDisplayEnd = computed<number>(() => {
    return Math.min(this.currentPage() * this.pageSize(), this.filteredRoles().length);
  });

  // Flat list of permissions for table
  flatPermissions = computed<FlatPermissionItem[]>(() => {
    const query = this.permissionFilterQuery().trim().toLowerCase();
    const flat: FlatPermissionItem[] = [];

    for (const mod of this.permissionModules()) {
      for (const p of mod.permissions) {
        if (
          !query ||
          p.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          p.description.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          p.action.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          mod.module_name.toLowerCase().includes(query)
        ) {
          flat.push({
            moduleKey: mod.module_key,
            moduleName: mod.module_name,
            action: p.action,
            name: p.name,
            description: p.description,
          });
        }
      }
    }
    return flat;
  });

  readonly Math = Math;

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadRoles();
    this.loadPermissionsRegistry();
  }

  loadRoles(): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.roleService.getAllRolesList().subscribe({
      next: (roles) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        this.rawRoles.set(roles);

        const current = this.selectedRole();
        if (current) {
          const fresh = roles.find((r) => r.id === current.id);
          if (fresh) {
            this.selectRole(fresh);
          }
        } else if (roles.length > 0 && !this.isCreateMode()) {
          this.selectRole(roles[0]);
        }
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظپط±ط§ط®ظˆط§ظ†غŒ ظ„غŒط³طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§.');
      },
    });
  }

  loadPermissionsRegistry(): void {
    this.roleService.getPermissionsRegistry().subscribe({
      next: (modules) => this.permissionModules.set(modules),
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  selectRole(role: RoleItem): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(false);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedRole.set(role);
    this.formEnglishName.set(role.name);
    this.formPersianName.set(role.description || role.name);

    // Load permissions for this role
    this.roleService.getRolePermissions(role.id).subscribe({
      next: (rules) => {
        const actions = new Set<string>();
        rules.forEach((r) => actions.add(r.action));
        this.roleSelectedActions.set(actions);
      },
      error: () => this.roleSelectedActions.set(new Set()),
    });

    // Load users for this role
    this.loadRoleUsers(role.id);
  }

  startEditRole(role: RoleItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) {
      event.stopPropagation();
    }
    this.selectRole(role);
    this.isEditMode.set(true);
  }

  enableEditMode(): void {
    this.isEditMode.set(true);
  }

  loadRoleUsers(roleId: string): void {
    this.isLoadingRoleUsers.set(true);
    this.roleService.getRoleUsers(roleId).subscribe({
      next: (users) => {
        this.isLoadingRoleUsers.set(false);
        this.roleUsers.set(users);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingRoleUsers.set(false);
        this.roleUsers.set([]);
      },
    });
  }

  startCreateNewRole(): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(true);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedRole.set(null);
    this.formEnglishName.set('');
    this.formPersianName.set('');
    this.roleSelectedActions.set(new Set());
    this.roleUsers.set([]);
    this.activeSubTab.set('permissions');
  }

  cancelDetailEdit(): void {
    if (this.isCreateMode()) {
      if (this.rawRoles().length > 0) {
        this.selectRole(this.rawRoles()[0]);
      } else {
        this.isCreateMode.set(false);
        this.selectedRole.set(null);
      }
    } else {
      const current = this.selectedRole();
      this.isEditMode.set(false);
      if (current) {
        this.selectRole(current);
      }
    }
  }

  onFilterChanged(): void {
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  goToPage(p: number): void {
    if (p >= 1 && p <= this.totalPages()) {
      this.currentPage.set(p);
    }
  }

  changePageSize(newSize: number): void {
    this.pageSize.set(newSize);
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  switchSubTab(tab: 'permissions' | 'users'): void {
    this.activeSubTab.set(tab);
  }

  // --- Checkbox toggle for permissions ---
  hasPermission(action: string): boolean {
    return this.roleSelectedActions().has(action);
  }

  togglePermission(action: string, event: Event): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) {
      return;
    }
    const checked = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
    const current = new Set(this.roleSelectedActions());
    if (checked) {
      current.add(action);
    } else {
      current.delete(action);
    }
    this.roleSelectedActions.set(current);
  }

  // --- Save / Submit Role ---
  saveRole(): void {
    const englishName = this.formEnglishName().trim();
    const persianName = this.formPersianName().trim();

    if (!englishName || !persianName) {
      this.showError('ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ظ‡ظ… ظ†ط§ظ… ط§ظ†ع¯ظ„غŒط³غŒ (ع©ط¯ ط³غŒط³طھظ…غŒ) ظˆ ظ‡ظ… ظ†ط§ظ… ظپط§ط±ط³غŒ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.');
      return;
    }

    this.isSaving.set(true);

    const permissionPayload = Array.from(this.roleSelectedActions()).map((act) => ({
      resource_type: 'GLOBAL',
      action: act,
      effect: 'ALLOW',
    }));

    if (this.isCreateMode()) {
      const createDto: RoleCreateDto = {
        name: englishName,
        description: persianName,
      };

      this.roleService.createRole(createDto).subscribe({
        next: (created) => {
          this.roleService.updateRolePermissions(created.id, permissionPayload).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              this.isSaving.set(false);
              this.isEditMode.set(false);
              this.showSuccess(`ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط³ط§ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ آ«${persianName}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯.`);
              this.loadRoles();
              this.selectRole(created);
            },
            error: () => {
              this.isSaving.set(false);
              this.isEditMode.set(false);
              this.showSuccess(`ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯طŒ ط§ظ…ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§ ط®ط·ط§غŒغŒ ط±ط® ط¯ط§ط¯.`);
              this.loadRoles();
              this.selectRole(created);
            },
          });
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isSaving.set(false);
          this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط«ط¨طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯.');
        },
      });
    } else {
      const role = this.selectedRole();
      if (!role) {
        this.isSaving.set(false);
        return;
      }

      const updateDto: RoleUpdateDto = {
        name: englishName,
        description: persianName,
      };

      this.roleService.updateRole(role.id, updateDto).subscribe({
        next: (updated) => {
          this.roleService.updateRolePermissions(updated.id, permissionPayload).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              this.isSaving.set(false);
              this.isEditMode.set(false);
              this.showSuccess(`ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ظˆ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§غŒ ظ†ظ‚ط´ آ«${persianName}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط¨ظ‡â€Œط±ظˆط²ط±ط³ط§ظ†غŒ ط´ط¯.`);
              this.loadRoles();
            },
            error: () => {
              this.isSaving.set(false);
              this.isEditMode.set(false);
              this.showError('ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯ ط§ظ…ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…ط¬ظˆط²ظ‡ط§ ط®ط·ط§غŒغŒ ط±ط® ط¯ط§ط¯.');
              this.loadRoles();
            },
          });
        },
        error: (err) => {
          this.isSaving.set(false);
          this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ظ†ظ‚ط´.');
        },
      });
    }
  }

  // Delete confirmation dialog signal
  readonly roleToDelete = signal<RoleItem | null>(null);

  requestDeleteRole(role: RoleItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) event.stopPropagation();
    if (role.is_system_role) {
      this.showError('ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط³غŒط³طھظ…غŒ ظˆ ظ¾غŒط´â€Œظپط±ط¶ ط³ط§ظ…ط§ظ†ظ‡ ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œط¨ط§ط´ظ†ط¯.');
      return;
    }
    this.roleToDelete.set(role);
  }

  cancelDeleteRole(): void {
    this.roleToDelete.set(null);
  }

  confirmDeleteRole(): void {
    const role = this.roleToDelete();
    if (!role) return;

    this.roleService.deleteRole(role.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.roleToDelete.set(null);
        this.showSuccess(`ظ†ظ‚ط´ آ«${role.description || role.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط­ط°ظپ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯.`);
        if (this.selectedRole()?.id === role.id) {
          this.selectedRole.set(null);
        }
        this.loadRoles();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.roleToDelete.set(null);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ظ‚ط´ ط³ط§ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ.');
      },
    });
  }

  deleteRole(role: RoleItem, event: Event): void {
    this.requestDeleteRole(role, event);
  }

  showSuccess(msg: string): void {
    this.successMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.successMessage.set(null), 4000);
  }

  showError(msg: string): void {
    this.errorMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.errorMessage.set(null), 5000);
  }

import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  DirectPermissionRule,
  GroupCreateDto,
  GroupItem,
  GroupMemberItem,
  GroupUpdateDto,
  PaginatedResponse,
} from '../models/access-control.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class GroupManagementService {
  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private baseUrl = '/api/v1/groups';

  getGroups(params: {
    page?: number;
    page_size?: number;
    search?: string;
    is_active?: boolean;
  }): Observable<PaginatedResponse<GroupItem>> {
    let httpParams = new HttpParams();
    if (params.page !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('page', params.page);
    if (params.page_size !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('page_size', params.page_size);
    if (params.search) httpParams = httpParams.set('search', params.search);
    if (params.is_active !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('is_active', params.is_active);

    return this.http.get<PaginatedResponse<GroupItem>>(this.baseUrl, { params: httpParams });
  }

  getGroup(id: string): Observable<GroupItem> {
    return this.http.get<GroupItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  createGroup(dto: GroupCreateDto): Observable<GroupItem> {
    return this.http.post<GroupItem>(this.baseUrl, dto);
  }

  updateGroup(id: string, dto: GroupUpdateDto): Observable<GroupItem> {
    return this.http.put<GroupItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`, dto);
  }

  deleteGroup(id: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  getGroupMembers(groupId: string): Observable<GroupMemberItem[]> {
    return this.http.get<GroupMemberItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/members`);
  }

  updateGroupMembers(groupId: string, userIds: string[]): Observable<GroupMemberItem[]> {
    return this.http.put<GroupMemberItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/members`, {
      user_ids: userIds,
    });
  }

  addGroupMember(groupId: string, userId: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.post<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/members/${userId}`, {});
  }

  removeGroupMember(groupId: string, userId: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/members/${userId}`);
  }

  getGroupRoles(groupId: string): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/roles`);
  }

  updateGroupRoles(groupId: string, roleIds: string[]): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.put<any[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/roles`, { role_ids: roleIds });
  }

  getGroupPermissions(groupId: string): Observable<DirectPermissionRule[]> {
    return this.http.get<DirectPermissionRule[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/permissions`);
  }

  bulkUpdateGroupPermissions(
    groupId: string,
    permissions: { resource_type: string; resource_id: string; action: string; effect: string }[]
  ): Observable<DirectPermissionRule[]> {
    return this.http.post<DirectPermissionRule[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${groupId}/permissions/bulk`, {
      permissions,
    });
  }
}

import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  DirectPermissionRule,
  EffectivePermissionRow,
  PaginatedResponse,
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  UserActivityItem,
  UserCreateDto,
  UserDetailData,
  UserItem,
  UserUpdateDto,
} from '../models/access-control.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class UserManagementService {
  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private baseUrl = '/api/v1/users';

  getUsers(params: {
    page?: number;
    page_size?: number;
    search?: string;
    role_id?: string;
    group_id?: string;
    status?: string;
  }): Observable<PaginatedResponse<UserItem>> {
    let httpParams = new HttpParams();
    if (params.page !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('page', params.page);
    if (params.page_size !== undefined) httpParams = httpParams.set('page_size', params.page_size);
    if (params.search) httpParams = httpParams.set('search', params.search);
    if (params.role_id) httpParams = httpParams.set('role_id', params.role_id);
    if (params.group_id) httpParams = httpParams.set('group_id', params.group_id);
    if (params.status) httpParams = httpParams.set('status', params.status);

    return this.http.get<PaginatedResponse<UserItem>>(this.baseUrl, { params: httpParams });
  }

  getUser(id: string): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.get<UserItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  getUserDetails(id: string): Observable<UserDetailData> {
    return this.http.get<UserDetailData>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/details`);
  }

  createUser(dto: UserCreateDto): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.post<UserItem>(this.baseUrl, dto);
  }

  updateUser(id: string, dto: UserUpdateDto): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.put<UserItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`, dto);
  }

  updateUserStatus(id: string, status: string): Observable<UserItem> {
    return this.http.patch<UserItem>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/status`, { status });
  }

  resetPassword(id: string, newPassword: string): Observable<{ message: string }> {
    return this.http.post<{ message: string }>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/reset-password`, {
      new_password: newPassword,
    });
  }

  deleteUser(id: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  getUserGroups(id: string): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/groups`);
  }

  updateUserGroups(id: string, groupIds: string[]): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.put<any[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/groups`, { group_ids: groupIds });
  }

  getUserDirectPermissions(id: string): Observable<DirectPermissionRule[]> {
    return this.http.get<DirectPermissionRule[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/permissions`);
  }

  bulkUpdateUserPermissions(
    id: string,
    permissions: { resource_type: string; resource_id?: string | null; action: string; effect: string }[]
  ): Observable<DirectPermissionRule[]> {
    return this.http.post<DirectPermissionRule[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/permissions/bulk`, {
      permissions,
    });
  }

  deleteUserPermissionRule(userId: string, ruleId: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${userId}/permissions/${ruleId}`);
  }

  getUserEffectivePermissions(
    id: string,
    search?: string,
    action?: string
  ): Observable<EffectivePermissionRow[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (search) params = params.set('search', search);
    if (action) params = params.set('action', action);
    return this.http.get<EffectivePermissionRow[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/effective-permissions`, {
      params,
    });
  }

  getUserActivity(id: string, limit: number = 50): Observable<UserActivityItem[]> {
    const params = new HttpParams().set('limit', limit);
    return this.http.get<UserActivityItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/activity`, { params });
  }

  getUserAssignedDatabases(userId: string): Observable<string[]> {
    return this.http.get<string[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${userId}/databases`);
  }

  updateUserAssignedDatabases(userId: string, databaseIds: string[]): Observable<string[]> {
    return this.http.put<string[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/${userId}/databases`, {
      database_ids: databaseIds,
    });
  }

  getPhysicalTree(): Observable<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]> {
    return this.http.get<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>('/api/v1/tables/tree/physical');
  }

  getCategoryTree(): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree');
  }

  getAllConnections(): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>('/api/v1/connections');
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { UserManagementService } from '../services/user-management.service';
import { RoleManagementService } from '../services/role-management.service';
import {
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  PhysicalSchemaNode,
  RoleItem,
  UserCreateDto,
  UserItem,
  UserUpdateDto,
} from '../models/access-control.models';

export function splitFullName(fullName: string): { firstName: string; lastName: string } {
  const parts = (fullName || '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length === 0) return { firstName: '', lastName: '' };
  if (parts.length === 1) return { firstName: parts[0], lastName: '' };
  if (parts.length === 2) return { firstName: parts[0], lastName: parts[1] };
  if (parts.length === 3) {
    return { firstName: `${parts[0]} ${parts[1]}`, lastName: parts[2] };
  }
  return {
    firstName: `${parts[0]} ${parts[1]}`,
    lastName: parts.slice(2).join(' '),
  };
}

export interface UserRowViewModel {
  user: UserItem;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-user-management',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterModule],

  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class UserManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  private userService = inject(UserManagementService);
  private roleService = inject(RoleManagementService);

  // Users grid state
  rawUsers = signal<UserItem[]>([]);
  totalUsers = signal<number>(0);
  currentPage = signal<number>(1);
  pageSize = signal<number>(5);
  availablePageSizes = [5, 10, 20, 50];

  // Search & Filter Toolbar
  searchTerm = signal<string>('');
  filterStatus = signal<string>('ALL');

  // Column search filters (legacy / direct signals)
  filterFirstName = signal<string>('');
  filterLastName = signal<string>('');
  filterUsername = signal<string>('');
  filterEmail = signal<string>('');

  isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  isSaving = signal<boolean>(false);
  errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  successMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Available roles for selection
  allRoles = signal<RoleItem[]>([]);
  selectedRoleToAdd = signal<string>('');

  // Category & Physical Databases Tree
  categoryTree = signal<any[]>([]);
  allConnections = signal<any[]>([]);
  physicalTree = signal<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>([]);
  expandedDbNodes = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());
  expandedCategoryIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Virtual branch for uncategorized databases
  displayCategoryTree = computed(() => {
    const tree = this.categoryTree();
    const conns = this.allConnections().length > 0
      ? this.allConnections()
      : this.physicalTree();
    if (!conns || conns.length === 0) return tree;

    const assignedDbIds = new Set<string>();
    const collectAssigned = (nodes: any[]) => {
      for (const node of nodes) {
        if (node.id === 'uncategorized') continue;
        if (node.databases && Array.isArray(node.databases)) {
          for (const db of node.databases) {
            if (db?.id) assignedDbIds.add(db.id);
          }
        }
        if (node.children && Array.isArray(node.children)) {
          collectAssigned(node.children);
        }
      }
    };
    collectAssigned(tree);

    const uncategorizedDbs = conns
      .filter((c: any) => !assignedDbIds.has(c.id))
      .map((c: any) => ({
        id: c.id,
        name: c.name,
        database_name: c.database_name,
        engine_type: c.engine_type,
      }));

    if (uncategorizedDbs.length > 0) {
      const uncategorizedNode = {
        id: 'uncategorized',
        name: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡ (Uncategorized)',
        description: 'ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ع©ظ‡ ط¨ظ‡ ظ‡غŒع† ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹غŒ ط§ظ†طھط³ط§ط¨ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§ظ†ط¯',
        databases: uncategorizedDbs,
        children: [],
      };
      return [...tree, uncategorizedNode];
    }

    return tree;
  });

  // Sub-tabs in bottom detail panel
  activeDetailTab = signal<'databases' | 'permissions'>('databases');

  // Direct 8-Module Administrative Permissions
  readonly directPermissionDefs = [
    { action: 'MANAGE_CATEGORIES', label: 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹ط§طھ ظˆ ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒâ€Œظ‡ط§', desc: 'طھط¹ط±غŒظپطŒ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ظˆ ط­ط°ظپ ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± ط¯ط±ط®طھغŒ ظ…ظˆط¶ظˆط¹ط§طھ' },
    { action: 'MANAGE_CONNECTIONS', label: 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡', desc: 'ط§ظپط²ظˆط¯ظ† ظˆ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ط§طھطµط§ظ„ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡' },

    const status = this.filterStatus();
    const fnFilter = this.filterFirstName().trim().toLowerCase();
    const lnFilter = this.filterLastName().trim().toLowerCase();
    const unFilter = this.filterUsername().trim().toLowerCase();
    const emFilter = this.filterEmail().trim().toLowerCase();

    return list
      .map((u) => {
        const { firstName, lastName } = splitFullName(u.full_name);
        return { user: u, firstName, lastName };
      })
      .filter((row) => {
        if (status && status !== 'ALL' && row.user.status !== status) {
          return false;
        }
        if (search) {
          const matchSearch =
            (row.firstName || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.lastName || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.user.username || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.user.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
            (row.user.full_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
          if (!matchSearch) return false;
        }
        if (fnFilter && !(row.firstName || '').toLowerCase().includes(fnFilter)) return false;
        if (lnFilter && !(row.lastName || '').toLowerCase().includes(lnFilter)) return false;
        if (unFilter && !(row.user.username || '').toLowerCase().includes(unFilter)) return false;
        if (emFilter && !(row.user.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(emFilter)) return false;
        return true;
      });
  });

  paginatedUsers = computed<UserRowViewModel[]>(() => {
    const all = this.filteredUsers();
    const start = (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize();
    return all.slice(start, start + this.pageSize());
  });

  totalPages = computed<number>(() => {
    const count = this.filteredUsers().length;
    return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(count / this.pageSize()));
  });

  pageDisplayStart = computed<number>(() => {
    if (this.filteredUsers().length === 0) return 0;
    return (this.currentPage() - 1) * this.pageSize() + 1;
  });

  pageDisplayEnd = computed<number>(() => {
    return Math.min(this.currentPage() * this.pageSize(), this.filteredUsers().length);
  });

  readonly Math = Math;

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadUsers();
    this.loadRoles();
    this.loadPhysicalTree();
  }

  loadUsers(): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.userService.getUsers({ page: 1, page_size: 1000 }).subscribe({
      next: (resp) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        const items = Array.isArray(resp) ? resp : resp.items || [];
        this.rawUsers.set(items);
        this.totalUsers.set(items.length);

        // If a user was previously selected, refresh selection
        const current = this.selectedUser();
        if (current) {
          const fresh = items.find((u) => u.id === current.id);
          if (fresh) {
            this.selectUser(fresh);
          }
        } else if (items.length > 0 && !this.isCreateMode()) {
          this.selectUser(items[0]);
        }
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ظ„غŒط³طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±ط§ظ†.');
      },
    });
  }

  loadRoles(): void {
    this.roleService.getAllRolesList().subscribe({
      next: (roles) => this.allRoles.set(roles),
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  loadPhysicalTree(): void {
    this.userService.getCategoryTree().subscribe({
      next: (tree) => {
        this.categoryTree.set(tree || []);
        const rootIds = new Set<string>();
        rootIds.add('uncategorized');
        const collectIds = (nodes: any[]) => {
          for (const n of nodes) {
            rootIds.add(n.id);
            if (n.children && n.children.length > 0) {
              collectIds(n.children);
            }
          }
        };
        collectIds(tree || []);
        this.expandedCategoryIds.set(rootIds);
      },
      error: () => {},
    });

    this.userService.getAllConnections().subscribe({
      next: (conns) => this.allConnections.set(conns || []),
      error: () => {},
    });

    this.userService.getPhysicalTree().subscribe({
      next: (tree) => {
        this.physicalTree.set(tree);
        const initial = new Set<string>();
        tree.forEach((db) => initial.add(`db:${db.id}`));
        this.expandedDbNodes.set(initial);
      },
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  toggleCategory(catId: string): void {
    const current = new Set(this.expandedCategoryIds());
    if (current.has(catId)) {
      current.delete(catId);
    } else {
      current.add(catId);
    }
    this.expandedCategoryIds.set(current);
  }

  toggleDirectPermission(action: string, event: Event): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const checked = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
    const current = new Set(this.userDirectPermissions());
    if (checked) {
      current.add(action);
    } else {
      current.delete(action);
    }
    this.userDirectPermissions.set(current);
  }

  selectUser(user: UserItem): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(false);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedUser.set(user);
    this.populateFormWithUser(user);
  }

  startEditUser(user: UserItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) {
      event.stopPropagation();
    }
    this.selectUser(user);
    this.isEditMode.set(true);
  }

  enableEditMode(): void {
    if (this.selectedUser()) {
      this.isEditMode.set(true);
    }
  }

  private populateFormWithUser(user: UserItem): void {
    const { firstName, lastName } = splitFullName(user.full_name);
    this.formFirstName.set(firstName);
    this.formLastName.set(lastName);
    this.formEmail.set(user.email);
    this.formUsername.set(user.username);
    this.formPhone.set(user.phone || '');
    this.formPassword.set('');
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set([...(user.role_ids || [])]);

    // Load assigned databases for this user
    this.userService.getUserAssignedDatabases(user.id).subscribe({
      next: (dbIds) => {
        this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(new Set(dbIds));
      },
      error: () => {
        this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(new Set());
      },
    });

    // Load direct permissions for this user
    this.userService.getUserDirectPermissions(user.id).subscribe({
      next: (rules) => {
        const perms = new Set<string>();
        rules
          .filter((r) => r.resource_type === 'GLOBAL')
          .forEach((r) => perms.add(r.action));
        this.userDirectPermissions.set(perms);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.userDirectPermissions.set(new Set());
      },
    });
  }

  startCreateNewUser(): void {
    this.isCreateMode.set(true);
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    this.selectedUser.set(null);
    this.formFirstName.set('');
    this.formLastName.set('');
    this.formEmail.set('');
    this.formUsername.set('');
    this.formPhone.set('');
    this.formPassword.set('');
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set([]);
    this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(new Set());
    this.userDirectPermissions.set(new Set());
    this.activeDetailTab.set('databases');
  }

  generateRandomPassword(): void {
    const chars = 'abcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789!@#$%';
    let pwd = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      pwd += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
    }
    this.formPassword.set(pwd);
  }

  cancelDetailEdit(): void {
    this.isEditMode.set(false);
    if (this.isCreateMode()) {
      this.isCreateMode.set(false);
      if (this.rawUsers().length > 0) {
        this.selectUser(this.rawUsers()[0]);
      } else {
        this.selectedUser.set(null);
      }
    } else if (this.selectedUser()) {
      this.populateFormWithUser(this.selectedUser()!);
    }
  }

  onFilterChanged(): void {
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  goToPage(p: number): void {
    if (p >= 1 && p <= this.totalPages()) {
      this.currentPage.set(p);
    }
  }

  changePageSize(newSize: number): void {
    this.pageSize.set(newSize);
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  // --- Role Management in Form ---
  addRoleToUser(roleId: string): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable() || !roleId) return;
    const current = new Set(this.formSelectedRoleIds());
    current.add(roleId);
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set(Array.from(current));
    this.selectedRoleToAdd.set('');
  }

  removeRoleFromUser(roleId: string): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const updated = this.formSelectedRoleIds().filter((id) => id !== roleId);
    this.formSelectedRoleIds.set(updated);
  }

  getRoleName(roleId: string): string {
    const role = this.allRoles().find((r) => r.id === roleId);
    return role ? (role.description || role.name) : roleId;
  }

  getAvailableRolesToAdd(): RoleItem[] {
    const assigned = new Set(this.formSelectedRoleIds());
    return this.allRoles().filter((r) => !assigned.has(r.id));
  }

  // --- Database Tree Toggle & Checkbox Actions ---
  toggleNodeExpanded(key: string): void {
    const current = new Set(this.expandedDbNodes());
    if (current.has(key)) {
      current.delete(key);
    } else {
      current.add(key);
    }
    this.expandedDbNodes.set(current);
  }

  isNodeExpanded(key: string): boolean {
    return this.expandedDbNodes().has(key);
  }

  isDatabaseSelected(dbId: string): boolean {
    return this.formSelectedDatabaseIds().has(dbId);
  }

  toggleDatabaseSelection(dbId: string, event: Event): void {
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const checked = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
    const updated = new Set(this.formSelectedDatabaseIds());
    if (checked) {
      updated.add(dbId);
    } else {
      updated.delete(dbId);
    }
    this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(updated);
  }

  getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat: any): string[] {
    const ids: string[] = [];
    const collect = (node: any) => {
      if (!node) return;
      if (node.databases && Array.isArray(node.databases)) {
        for (const db of node.databases) {
          if (db && db.id) {
            ids.push(db.id);
          }
        }
      }
      if (node.children && Array.isArray(node.children)) {
        for (const child of node.children) {
          collect(child);
        }
      }
    };
    collect(cat);
    return ids;
  }

  isCategoryChecked(cat: any): boolean {
    const dbIds = this.getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat);
    if (dbIds.length === 0) return false;
    const selected = this.formSelectedDatabaseIds();
    return dbIds.every((id) => selected.has(id));
  }

  isCategoryIndeterminate(cat: any): boolean {
    const dbIds = this.getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat);
    if (dbIds.length === 0) return false;
    const selected = this.formSelectedDatabaseIds();
    let selectedCount = 0;
    for (const id of dbIds) {
      if (selected.has(id)) {
        selectedCount++;
      }
    }
    return selectedCount > 0 && selectedCount < dbIds.length;
  }

  toggleCategorySelection(cat: any, event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    if (!this.isFormEditable()) return;
    const target = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const checked = target.checked;
    const dbIds = this.getDescendantDatabaseIds(cat);
    if (dbIds.length === 0) return;

    const updated = new Set(this.formSelectedDatabaseIds());
    if (checked) {
      dbIds.forEach((id) => updated.add(id));
    } else {
      dbIds.forEach((id) => updated.delete(id));
    }
    this.formSelectedDatabaseIds.set(updated);
  }

        next: (created) => {

  // --- Row Actions: Delete & Status ---
  toggleUserStatus(user: UserItem, event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    const nextStatus = user.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'SUSPENDED' : 'ACTIVE';
    this.userService.updateUserStatus(user.id, nextStatus).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.showSuccess(`ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± آ«${user.full_name}آ» ط¨ظ‡ ${nextStatus === 'ACTIVE' ? 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„' : 'ظ…ط¹ظ„ظ‚'} طھط؛غŒغŒط± غŒط§ظپطھ.`);
        this.loadUsers();
      },
      error: (err) => this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±.'),
    });
  }

  // Delete confirmation dialog signal
  readonly userToDelete = signal<UserItem | null>(null);

  requestDeleteUser(user: UserItem, event?: Event): void {
    if (event) event.stopPropagation();
    if (user.is_superadmin) {
      this.showError('ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ظ…ط¯غŒط± ط§ط±ط´ط¯ ط³غŒط³طھظ… (SuperAdmin) ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†غŒط³طھ.');
      return;
    }
    this.userToDelete.set(user);
  }

  cancelDeleteUser(): void {
    this.userToDelete.set(null);
  }

  confirmDeleteUser(): void {
    const user = this.userToDelete();
    if (!user) return;

    this.userService.deleteUser(user.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.userToDelete.set(null);
        this.showSuccess(`ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ آ«${user.username}آ» ط­ط°ظپ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯.`);
        if (this.selectedUser()?.id === user.id) {
          this.selectedUser.set(null);
        }
        this.loadUsers();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.userToDelete.set(null);
        this.showError(err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط­ط°ظپ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±.');
      },
    });
  }

  deleteUser(user: UserItem, event: Event): void {
    this.requestDeleteUser(user, event);
  }

  // --- Helpers ---
  showSuccess(msg: string): void {
    this.successMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.successMessage.set(null), 4000);
  }

  showError(msg: string): void {
    this.errorMessage.set(msg);
    setTimeout(() => this.errorMessage.set(null), 5000);
  }
}

import { KeysetPageInfo } from '../../catalog/models/catalog.models';

export interface AuditLogItem {
  id: string;
  actor_id?: string | null;
  actor_username: string;
  ip_address?: string | null;
  user_agent?: string | null;
  action: string;
  entity_type: string;
  entity_id: string;
  diff_jsonb: Record<string, any>;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface AuditLogResponse {
  items: AuditLogItem[];
  page_info: KeysetPageInfo;
}

export interface AuditLogFilterParams {
  entity_type?: string;
  entity_id?: string;
  actor_id?: string;
  action?: string;
  start_time?: string;
  end_time?: string;
  cursor?: string;
  limit?: number;
}


=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\audit\services\audit.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { AuditLogFilterParams, AuditLogResponse } from '../models/audit.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class AuditService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/audit-logs';

  /**
   * Retrieves keyset-paginated immutable audit logs with multi-column filtering.
   */
  getAuditLogs(filters?: AuditLogFilterParams): Observable<AuditLogResponse> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (filters) {
      if (filters.entity_type) {
        params = params.set('entity_type', filters.entity_type);
      }
      if (filters.entity_id) {
        params = params.set('entity_id', filters.entity_id);
      }
      if (filters.actor_id) {
        params = params.set('actor_id', filters.actor_id);
      }
      if (filters.action) {
        params = params.set('action', filters.action);
      }
      if (filters.start_time) {
        params = params.set('start_time', filters.start_time);
      }
      if (filters.end_time) {
        params = params.set('end_time', filters.end_time);
      }
      if (filters.cursor) {
        params = params.set('cursor', filters.cursor);
      }
      if (filters.limit !== undefined) {
        params = params.set('limit', filters.limit.toString());
      }
    }

    return this.http.get<AuditLogResponse>(this.baseUrl, { params });
  }
}

export class LoginComponent {
  private readonly authService = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  username = '';
  password = '';
  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  onLogin(): void {
    if (!this.username || !this.password) return;
    this.executeAuth(this.username, this.password);
  }

  loginWithDemo(u: string, p: string): void {
    this.username = u;
    this.password = p;
    this.executeAuth(u, p);
  }

  private executeAuth(username: string, pass: string): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.authService.login({ username_or_email: username, password: pass }).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        this.router.navigate(['/catalog']);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoading.set(false);
        const msg = err?.error?.detail || 'ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ غŒط§ ع¯ط°ط±ظˆط§عکظ‡ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط§ط³طھ.';
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
      },
    });
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\catalog\components\inline-desc-editor\inline-desc-editor.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  effect,
  input,
  output,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { TagChipComponent } from '../tag-chip/tag-chip.component';
import { TagItem } from '../../models/catalog.models';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-inline-desc-editor',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, TagChipComponent],
  templateUrl: './inline-desc-editor.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./inline-desc-editor.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class InlineDescEditorComponent {
  readonly description = input<string | null | undefined>(null);
  readonly placeholder = input<string>('No description provided. Click to add.');
  readonly canEdit = input<boolean>(true);
  readonly canManageTags = input<boolean>(false);
  readonly tags = input<TagItem[]>([]);
  readonly isLoading = input<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = input<string | null>(null);

  readonly isEditing = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly currentDescription = signal<string>('');
  readonly editValue = signal<string>('');

  readonly saved = output<string>();
  readonly cancelled = output<void>();
  readonly tagRemoved = output<TagItem>();

  constructor() {
    effect(() => {
      const desc = this.description();
      this.currentDescription.set(desc ?? '');
    });
  }

  startEdit(): void {
    if (!this.canEdit()) return;
    this.editValue.set(this.currentDescription());
    this.isEditing.set(true);
  }

  onInputChange(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLTextAreaElement;
    this.editValue.set(target.value);
  }

  save(): void {
    const trimmed = this.editValue().trim();
    this.currentDescription.set(trimmed);
    this.isEditing.set(false);
    this.saved.emit(trimmed);
  }

  cancelEdit(): void {
    this.editValue.set(this.currentDescription());
    this.isEditing.set(false);
    this.cancelled.emit();
  }

  onTagRemove(tag: TagItem): void {
    this.tagRemoved.emit(tag);
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\catalog\components\table-virtual-scroll\table-virtual-scroll.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  EventEmitter,
  Output,
  computed,
  inject,
  input,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { ScrollingModule } from '@angular/cdk/scrolling';
import { CatalogStore } from '../../stores/catalog.store';
import { SearchHit } from '../../models/catalog.models';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-table-virtual-scroll',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ScrollingModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './table-virtual-scroll.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./table-virtual-scroll.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class TableVirtualScrollComponent {
  readonly store = inject(CatalogStore);

  /**
   * Optional custom items input. Defaults to store.searchHits() if not provided.
   */
  readonly itemsInput = input<SearchHit[] | undefined>(undefined, { alias: 'items' });

  /**
   * Reactive signal resolving items to render.
   */
  readonly items = computed<SearchHit[]>(() => this.itemsInput() ?? this.store.searchHits());

  /**
   * Emitted when user scrolls near the end of the loaded catalog items.
   */
  @Output() readonly nearBottom = new EventEmitter<void>();

  /**
   * Deterministic trackBy function using entity UUID.
   */
  trackById(index: number, item: SearchHit): string {
    return item.id;
  }

  /**
   * Handles scroll position changes from cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport.
   * Automatically triggers keyset pagination when approaching the end of current dataset.
   */
  onScroll(scrolledIndex: number): void {
    const total = this.items().length;
    // Trigger when user is within 15 items of current end
    if (total > 0 && scrolledIndex + 15 >= total) {
      if (this.store.hasMore() && !this.store.isLoading()) {
        this.store.loadNextPage();
        this.nearBottom.emit();
      }
    }
  }
}

  output,
} from '@angular/core';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-tag-chip',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  templateUrl: './tag-chip.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./tag-chip.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class TagChipComponent {
  readonly tag = input.required<TagItem>();
  readonly removable = input<boolean>(false);

=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\catalog\stores\catalog.store.ts ===
import { Injectable, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { firstValueFrom } from 'rxjs';
import {
  BulkTagRequest,
  CategoryTreeNode,
  ChangeLogItem,
  ColumnItem,
  KeysetPageInfo,
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  SearchHit,
  SearchResponse,
  TableDetail,
  TableItem,
} from '../models/catalog.models';

export interface CatalogFilterOptions {
  database?: string;
  schema?: string;
  classification?: string;
  status?: string;
  tag?: string;
  category?: string;
  missing_descriptions_only?: boolean;
}

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class CatalogStore {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  // Private state signals
  private readonly _tables = signal<TableItem[]>([]);
  private readonly _searchHits = signal<SearchHit[]>([]);
  private readonly _pageInfo = signal<KeysetPageInfo | null>(null);
  private readonly _isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  private readonly _activeFilter = signal<string>('');
  private readonly _activeFilterOptions = signal<CatalogFilterOptions>({});
  private readonly _error = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Public readonly selectors
  readonly tables = this._tables.asReadonly();
  readonly searchHits = this._searchHits.asReadonly();
  readonly isLoading = this._isLoading.asReadonly();
  readonly pageInfo = this._pageInfo.asReadonly();
  readonly activeFilter = this._activeFilter.asReadonly();
  readonly activeFilterOptions = this._activeFilterOptions.asReadonly();
  readonly error = this._error.asReadonly();

  // Computed signals
  readonly hasMore = computed<boolean>(() => this._pageInfo()?.has_next_page ?? false);
  readonly totalHitCount = computed<number>(() => this._searchHits().length);

  /**
   * Executes keyset-paginated search against the catalog API.
   * If reset is true, clears accumulated results and starts from beginning.
   */
  async search(
    query: string = '',
    reset: boolean = true,
    filters?: CatalogFilterOptions
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (reset) {
      this._searchHits.set([]);
      this._pageInfo.set(null);
      this._activeFilter.set(query);
      if (filters !== undefined) {
        this._activeFilterOptions.set(filters);
      }
    }

    this._isLoading.set(true);
    this._error.set(null);

    const activeOpts = filters !== undefined ? filters : this._activeFilterOptions();
    const cursor = reset ? '' : (this._pageInfo()?.next_cursor || '');
    let url = `/api/v1/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=50&cursor=${cursor}`;

    if (activeOpts.database) {
      url += `&database=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.database)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.schema) {
      url += `&schema=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.schema)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.classification) {
      url += `&classification=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.classification)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.status) {
      url += `&status=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.status)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.tag) {
      url += `&tag=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.tag)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.category) {
      url += `&category=${encodeURIComponent(activeOpts.category)}`;
    }
    if (activeOpts.missing_descriptions_only) {
      url += `&missing_descriptions_only=true`;
    }

    try {
      const resp = await firstValueFrom(this.http.get<SearchResponse>(url));
      this._searchHits.update(current => (reset ? resp.items : [...current, ...resp.items]));
      this._pageInfo.set(resp.page_info);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Catalog search request failed';
      this._error.set(msg);
      throw err;
    } finally {
      this._isLoading.set(false);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Appends the next keyset page to the existing search results.
   */
  async loadNextPage(): Promise<void> {
    if (this._isLoading() || !this.hasMore()) {
      return;
    }
    const currentQuery = this._activeFilter();
    await this.search(currentQuery, false);
  }

  /**
   * Updates approved description for a table and synchronizes reactive state.
   */
  async updateDescription(tableId: string, approvedDescription: string): Promise<void> {
    const updated = await firstValueFrom(
      this.http.patch<{ id: string; effective_description: string }>(
        `/api/v1/tables/${tableId}/description`,
        { approved_description: approvedDescription }
      )
    );

    const newDesc = updated.effective_description;

    this._tables.update(list =>
      list.map(t => (t.id === tableId ? { ...t, effective_description: newDesc } : t))
    );

    this._searchHits.update(list =>
      list.map(hit => (hit.id === tableId ? { ...hit, effective_description: newDesc } : hit))
    );
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves full table details including columns and tags.
   */
  async getTableDetails(tableId: string): Promise<TableDetail> {
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<TableDetail>(`/api/v1/tables/${tableId}`));
  }

  /**
   * Approves AI-suggested description for a table.
   */
  async approveAiTableDescription(tableId: string): Promise<TableDetail> {
    const updated = await firstValueFrom(
      this.http.post<TableDetail>(`/api/v1/tables/${tableId}/approve-ai-description`, {})
    );
    this._tables.update((list) =>
      list.map((t) => (t.id === tableId ? { ...t, effective_description: updated.effective_description } : t))
    );
    this._searchHits.update((list) =>
      list.map((hit) => (hit.id === tableId ? { ...hit, effective_description: updated.effective_description } : hit))
    );
    return updated;
  }

  /**
   * Approves AI-suggested description for a column.
   */
  async approveAiColumnDescription(columnId: string): Promise<ColumnItem> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.post<ColumnItem>(`/api/v1/columns/${columnId}/approve-ai-description`, {})
    );
  }

  /**
   * Updates approved description for a column.
   */
  async updateColumnDescription(columnId: string, approvedDescription: string): Promise<ColumnItem> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.patch<ColumnItem>(`/api/v1/columns/${columnId}/description`, {
        approved_description: approvedDescription,
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Updates security classification for a column.
   */
  async updateColumnClassification(columnId: string, classificationLevel: string): Promise<ColumnItem> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.patch<ColumnItem>(`/api/v1/columns/${columnId}/classification`, {
        classification_level: classificationLevel,
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Fetches change log history for a table.
   */
  async getTableHistory(tableId: string): Promise<ChangeLogItem[]> {
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<ChangeLogItem[]>(`/api/v1/tables/${tableId}/change-logs`));
  }

  /**
   * Fetches physical database tree.
   */
  async getPhysicalTree(): Promise<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]> {
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>('/api/v1/tables/tree/physical'));
  }

  /**
   * Fetches category business hierarchy tree.
   */
  async getCategoryTree(): Promise<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return firstValueFrom(this.http.get<CategoryTreeNode[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree'));
  }

  async loadCategoryTree(): Promise<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return this.getCategoryTree();
  }

  /**
   * Executes bulk tag assignment across entities.
   */
  async bulkTag(request: BulkTagRequest): Promise<{ success: boolean; affected_count: number }> {
    return firstValueFrom(
      this.http.post<{ success: boolean; affected_count: number }>('/api/v1/tags/bulk-assign', request)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Sets searchHits directly (useful for testing and component state priming).
   */
  setSearchHits(hits: SearchHit[]): void {
    this._searchHits.set(hits);
  }

  /**
   * Sets tables directly.
   */
  setTables(tables: TableItem[]): void {
    this._tables.set(tables);
  }

  /**
   * Resets all store state to initial defaults.
   */
  reset(): void {
    this._tables.set([]);
    this._searchHits.set([]);
    this._pageInfo.set(null);
    this._isLoading.set(false);
    this._activeFilter.set('');
    this._activeFilterOptions.set({});
    this._error.set(null);
  }
}
