import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { ScrollingModule } from '@angular/cdk/scrolling';
import { CatalogStore } from './stores/catalog.store';
import {
  CategoryTreeNode,
  ChangeLogItem,
  ColumnItem,
  PhysicalDatabaseNode,
  SearchHit,
  TableDetail,
  TagItem,
} from './models/catalog.models';
import { InlineDescEditorComponent } from './components/inline-desc-editor/inline-desc-editor.component';
import { TagChipComponent } from './components/tag-chip/tag-chip.component';
import { HasPermissionDirective } from '../../core/directives/has-permission.directive';
import { RbacService } from '../../core/auth/rbac.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-catalog-explorer',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    FormsModule,
    ScrollingModule,
    InlineDescEditorComponent,
    TagChipComponent,
    HasPermissionDirective,
  ],
  templateUrl: './catalog-explorer.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./catalog-explorer.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class CatalogExplorerComponent implements OnInit {
  readonly store = inject(CatalogStore);
  readonly rbac = inject(RbacService);
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  // Search & Filter State
  searchQuery = '';
  selectedDatabase = '';
  selectedSchema = '';
  selectedClassification = '';
  selectedStatus = '';
  selectedTag = '';
  selectedCategory = '';
  missingDescriptionsOnly = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Trees State
  activeTreeTab = signal<'physical' | 'category'>('physical');
  physicalTree = signal<PhysicalDatabaseNode[]>([]);
  categoryTree = signal<CategoryTreeNode[]>([]);
  isTreeLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  isSidebarExpanded = signal<boolean>(true);

  // Multi-selection for bulk tagging
  selectedTableIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Table Detail Inspector Drawer
  selectedTable = signal<TableDetail | null>(null);
  selectedTableId = signal<string | null>(null);
  activeInspectorTab = signal<'columns' | 'metadata' | 'history'>('columns');
  isInspectorLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  tableHistory = signal<ChangeLogItem[]>([]);

  // Bulk Ingestion Modal
  isIngestionModalOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  ingestConnectionId = signal<string>('');
  ingestSchemaName = signal<string>('analytics_public');
  ingestJsonPayload = signal<string>(JSON.stringify([
    {
      name: 'fact_order_items',
      table_type: 'TABLE',
      row_count_estimate: 250000,
      size_bytes: 45000000,
      source_description: 'ط¢غŒطھظ…â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظپط§ع©طھظˆط± ظپط±ظˆط´ ط§ط³طھط®ط±ط§ط¬ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط² ط¯ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ',
      columns: [
        { name: 'item_id', ordinal_position: 1, data_type: 'BIGINT', raw_data_type: 'bigint', is_primary_key: true, classification_level: 'INTERNAL' },
        { name: 'order_id', ordinal_position: 2, data_type: 'BIGINT', raw_data_type: 'bigint', is_foreign_key: true, classification_level: 'INTERNAL' },
        { name: 'product_sku', ordinal_position: 3, data_type: 'VARCHAR(50)', raw_data_type: 'varchar(50)', classification_level: 'INTERNAL' },
        { name: 'unit_price_cents', ordinal_position: 4, data_type: 'NUMERIC(12,2)', raw_data_type: 'numeric(12,2)', classification_level: 'INTERNAL' }
      ]
    }
  ], null, 2));

  // Bulk Tag Modal
  isBulkTagModalOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  availableTags = signal<TagItem[]>([]);
  selectedTagIdToAssign = signal<string>('');
  bulkTagAction = signal<'ASSIGN' | 'REMOVE'>('ASSIGN');

  // Tag Assignment to Column Modal
  isColumnTagModalOpen = signal<boolean>(false);
  targetColumn = signal<ColumnItem | null>(null);
  selectedTagForColumn = signal<string>('');

  // Toast Notification
  toastMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  toastType = signal<'success' | 'error' | 'info'>('success');

  // Computed
  hasSelectedTables = computed(() => this.selectedTableIds().size > 0);
  selectedCount = computed(() => this.selectedTableIds().size);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    // 1. Initial Catalog Load
    this.executeSearch();

    // 2. Load Hierarchy Trees
    this.loadTrees();

    // 3. Load Available Tags
    this.loadAvailableTags();
  }

  executeSearch(): void {
    const filters = {
      database: this.selectedDatabase || undefined,
      schema: this.selectedSchema || undefined,
      classification: this.selectedClassification || undefined,
      status: this.selectedStatus || undefined,
      tag: this.selectedTag || undefined,
      category: this.selectedCategory || undefined,
      missing_descriptions_only: this.missingDescriptionsOnly() || undefined,
    };
    this.store.search(this.searchQuery, true, filters).catch(() => {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط±ظ‚ط±ط§ط±غŒ ط§ط±طھط¨ط§ط· ط¨ط§ ط³ط±ظˆط± ظ…طھط§ط¯غŒطھط§', 'error');
    });
  }

  toggleMissingDescriptionsOnly(): void {
    this.missingDescriptionsOnly.update((v) => !v);
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  resetFilters(): void {
    this.searchQuery = '';
    this.selectedDatabase = '';
    this.selectedSchema = '';
    this.selectedClassification = '';
    this.selectedStatus = '';
    this.selectedTag = '';
    this.selectedCategory = '';
    this.missingDescriptionsOnly.set(false);
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  clearSelection(): void {
    this.selectedTableIds.set(new Set());
  }

  async loadTrees(): Promise<void> {
    this.isTreeLoading.set(true);
    try {
      const [phys, cat] = await Promise.all([
        this.store.getPhysicalTree(),
        this.store.getCategoryTree(),
      ]);
      this.physicalTree.set(phys);
      this.categoryTree.set(cat);
      if (phys.length > 0 && !this.ingestConnectionId()) {
        this.ingestConnectionId.set(phys[0].id);
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignored or handled gracefully
    } finally {
      this.isTreeLoading.set(false);
    }
  }

  loadAvailableTags(): void {
    this.http.get<TagItem[]>('/api/v1/tags').subscribe({
      next: (tags) => {
        this.availableTags.set(tags);
        if (tags.length > 0 && !this.selectedTagIdToAssign()) {
          this.selectedTagIdToAssign.set(tags[0].id);
        }
      },
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  // Tree Node Selection Handlers
  filterByDatabase(databaseName: string): void {
    this.selectedDatabase = databaseName;
    this.selectedSchema = '';
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  filterBySchema(schemaName: string): void {
    this.selectedSchema = schemaName;
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  filterByCategory(categoryIdentifier: string): void {
    this.selectedCategory = categoryIdentifier;
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  filterByTable(tableName: string): void {
    this.searchQuery = tableName;
    this.executeSearch();
  }

  // Selection Checkbox Handlers
  toggleTableSelection(id: string): void {
    this.selectedTableIds.update((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (next.has(id)) {
        next.delete(id);
      } else {
        next.add(id);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  toggleSelectAll(): void {
    const hits = this.store.searchHits();
    const current = this.selectedTableIds();
    if (current.size === hits.length && hits.length > 0) {
      this.selectedTableIds.set(new Set());
    } else {
      this.selectedTableIds.set(new Set(hits.map((h) => h.id)));
    }
  }

  isTableSelected(id: string): boolean {
    return this.selectedTableIds().has(id);
  }

  // Table Detail Inspector Drawer
  async inspectTable(tableId: string): Promise<void> {
    this.selectedTableId.set(tableId);
    this.isInspectorLoading.set(true);
    try {
      const detail = await this.store.getTableDetails(tableId);
      this.selectedTable.set(detail);
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(tableId);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط¹ط¯ظ… ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ غŒط§ ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط´ظ†ط§ط³ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„', 'error');
      this.selectedTable.set(null);
    } finally {
      this.isInspectorLoading.set(false);
    }
  }

  closeInspector(): void {
    this.selectedTable.set(null);
    this.selectedTableId.set(null);
  }

  // AI Suggestion Approvals
  async approveAiTableDescription(tableId: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.approveAiTableDescription(tableId);
      this.selectedTable.set(updated);
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(tableId);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast('ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظˆ ط¨ظ‡ ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ط´ط±ط­ ظ†ظ‡ط§غŒغŒ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯', 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ', 'error');
    }
  }

  async approveAiColumnDescription(col: ColumnItem): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.approveAiColumnDescription(col.id);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => {
        if (!curr) return null;
        return {
          ...curr,
          columns: curr.columns.map((c) => (c.id === col.id ? updated : c)),
        };
      });
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(col.table_id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast(`ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط³طھظˆظ† آ«${col.name}آ» طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯`, 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ‡ظˆط´ ظ…طµظ†ظˆط¹غŒ ط³طھظˆظ†', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Table Approved Description Editor
  async onSaveTableDescription(newDesc: string): Promise<void> {
    const tbl = this.selectedTable();
    if (!tbl) return;
    try {
      await this.store.updateDescription(tbl.id, newDesc);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => (curr ? { ...curr, approved_description: newDesc, effective_description: newDesc } : curr));
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(tbl.id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast('طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظˆ ط¯ط± ط²ظ†ط¬غŒط±ظ‡ طھط؛غŒغŒط±ط§طھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯', 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Column Approved Description Editor
  async onSaveColumnDescription(col: ColumnItem, newDesc: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.updateColumnDescription(col.id, newDesc);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => {
        if (!curr) return null;
        return {
          ...curr,
          columns: curr.columns.map((c) => (c.id === col.id ? updated : c)),
        };
      });
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(col.table_id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast(`طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ظپغŒظ„ط¯ ${col.name} ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯`, 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط³طھظˆظ†', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Column Classification Dropdown Update
  async onClassificationChange(col: ColumnItem, newLevel: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const updated = await this.store.updateColumnClassification(col.id, newLevel);
      this.selectedTable.update((curr) => {
        if (!curr) return null;
        return {
          ...curr,
          columns: curr.columns.map((c) => (c.id === col.id ? updated : c)),
        };
      });
      const history = await this.store.getTableHistory(col.table_id);
      this.tableHistory.set(history);
      this.showToast(`ط³ط·ط­ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…ط§ظ†ع¯غŒ ظپغŒظ„ط¯ ${col.name} ط¨ظ‡ ${newLevel} طھط؛غŒغŒط± غŒط§ظپطھ`, 'success');
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط؛غŒغŒط± ط³ط·ط­ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…ط§ظ†ع¯غŒ', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Column Tag Assignment
  openColumnTagModal(col: ColumnItem): void {
    this.targetColumn.set(col);
    if (this.availableTags().length > 0) {
      this.selectedTagForColumn.set(this.availableTags()[0].id);
    }
    this.isColumnTagModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeColumnTagModal(): void {
    this.isColumnTagModalOpen.set(false);
    this.targetColumn.set(null);
  }

  async assignTagToColumn(): Promise<void> {
    const col = this.targetColumn();
    const tagId = this.selectedTagForColumn();
    if (!col || !tagId) return;

    try {
      await this.store.bulkTag({
        tag_ids: [tagId],
        action: 'ASSIGN',
        targets: [{ entity_type: 'COLUMN', entity_id: col.id }],
      });
      this.showToast('طھع¯ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط¨ظ‡ ظپغŒظ„ط¯ طھط®طµغŒطµ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯', 'success');
      this.closeColumnTagModal();
      // Reload table details to reflect tags
      const detail = await this.store.getTableDetails(col.table_id);
      this.selectedTable.set(detail);
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط®طµغŒطµ طھع¯ ط¨ظ‡ ظپغŒظ„ط¯', 'error');
    }
  }

  async removeTagFromColumn(col: ColumnItem, tag: TagItem): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.store.bulkTag({
        tag_ids: [tag.id],
        action: 'REMOVE',
        targets: [{ entity_type: 'COLUMN', entity_id: col.id }],
      });
      this.showToast(`طھع¯ ${tag.name} ط§ط² ظپغŒظ„ط¯ ط­ط°ظپ ط´ط¯`, 'info');
      const detail = await this.store.getTableDetails(col.table_id);
      this.selectedTable.set(detail);
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط­ط°ظپ طھع¯', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Bulk Tagging Handlers
  openBulkTagModal(): void {
    this.isBulkTagModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeBulkTagModal(): void {
    this.isBulkTagModalOpen.set(false);
  }

  async executeBulkTag(): Promise<void> {
    const tableIds = Array.from(this.selectedTableIds());
    const tagId = this.selectedTagIdToAssign();
    if (tableIds.length === 0 || !tagId) return;

    try {
      const res = await this.store.bulkTag({
        tag_ids: [tagId],
        action: this.bulkTagAction(),
        targets: tableIds.map((tid) => ({ entity_type: 'TABLE', entity_id: tid })),
      });
      this.showToast(`ط¹ظ…ظ„غŒط§طھ طھع¯ ط±ظˆغŒ ${res.affected_count} ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯`, 'success');
      this.closeBulkTagModal();
      this.selectedTableIds.set(new Set());
      this.executeSearch();
    } catch {
      this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒ طھط®طµغŒطµ ع¯ط±ظˆظ‡غŒ طھع¯', 'error');
    }
  }

  // Bulk Ingestion Handlers
  openIngestionModal(): void {
    this.isIngestionModalOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeIngestionModal(): void {
    this.isIngestionModalOpen.set(false);
  }

  async executeBulkIngestion(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const tablesParsed = JSON.parse(this.ingestJsonPayload());
      const payload = {
        connection_id: this.ingestConnectionId(),
        schema_name: this.ingestSchemaName(),
        tables: tablesParsed,
      };

      await this.http.post('/api/v1/ingestion/bulk', payload).toPromise();
      this.showToast('ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ظ…طھط§ط¯غŒطھط§ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ ظˆ ظ†ط³ط®ظ‡ ط§ط³ع©غŒظ…ط§ ط§ط±طھظ‚ط§ غŒط§ظپطھ', 'success');
      this.closeIngestionModal();
      this.executeSearch();
      this.loadTrees();
    } catch (err: any) {
      const msg = err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± ظ…طھط§ط¯غŒطھط§. ط§ط² ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط¨ظˆط¯ظ† ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± JSON ط§ط·ظ…غŒظ†ط§ظ† ط­ط§طµظ„ ظپط±ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.';
      this.showToast(msg, 'error');
    }
  }

  // Toast Helper
  showToast(message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' | 'info' = 'success'): void {
    this.toastMessage.set(message);
    this.toastType.set(type);
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.toastMessage.set(null);
    }, 4000);
  }

  trackByHitId(index: number, hit: SearchHit): string {
    return hit.id;
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\connections\services\connection.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  ConnectionCreateRequest,
  ConnectionTestResult,
  DatabaseConnection,
} from '../models/connection.models';
import { CategoryTreeNode } from '../../catalog/models/catalog.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class ConnectionService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/connections';

  /**
   * Retrieves business category hierarchy tree.
   */
  getCategoryTree(): Observable<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return this.http.get<CategoryTreeNode[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree');
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves all registered database connections with optional active filter.
   */
  getConnections(isActive?: boolean): Observable<DatabaseConnection[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (isActive !== undefined) {
      params = params.set('is_active', isActive.toString());
    }
    return this.http.get<DatabaseConnection[]>(this.baseUrl, { params });
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves single database connection by ID.
   */
  getConnection(id: string): Observable<DatabaseConnection> {
    return this.http.get<DatabaseConnection>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  /**
   * Registers a new database connection with envelope encrypted credentials.
   */
  createConnection(request: ConnectionCreateRequest): Observable<DatabaseConnection> {
    return this.http.post<DatabaseConnection>(this.baseUrl, request);
  }

  /**
   * Updates an existing database connection.
   */
  updateConnection(id: string, request: Partial<ConnectionCreateRequest>): Observable<DatabaseConnection> {
    return this.http.put<DatabaseConnection>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`, request);
  }

  /**
   * Deletes a database connection.
   */
  deleteConnection(id: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  /**
   * Executes live dialect connection ping test with 5s timeout.
   */
  testConnection(id: string): Observable<ConnectionTestResult> {
    return this.http.post<ConnectionTestResult>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}/test`, {});
  }
}


=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\connections\validators\cron.validator.ts ===
import { AbstractControl, ValidationErrors, ValidatorFn } from '@angular/forms';

const MONTH_NAMES: Record<string, number> = {
  JAN: 1,
  FEB: 2,
  MAR: 3,
  APR: 4,
  MAY: 5,
  JUN: 6,
  JUL: 7,
  AUG: 8,
  SEP: 9,
  OCT: 10,
  NOV: 11,
  DEC: 12,
};

const DOW_NAMES: Record<string, number> = {
  SUN: 0,
  MON: 1,
  TUE: 2,
  WED: 3,
  THU: 4,
  FRI: 5,
  SAT: 6,
};

interface FieldBound {
  name: string;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  nameMap?: Record<string, number>;
}

const FIELD_BOUNDS: FieldBound[] = [
  { name: 'minute', min: 0, max: 59 },
  { name: 'hour', min: 0, max: 23 },
  { name: 'day of month', min: 1, max: 31 },
  { name: 'month', min: 1, max: 12, nameMap: MONTH_NAMES },
  { name: 'day of week', min: 0, max: 7, nameMap: DOW_NAMES },
];

function parseToken(token: string, nameMap?: Record<string, number>): number {
  const upper = token.toUpperCase();
  if (nameMap && upper in nameMap) {
    return nameMap[upper];
  }
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(token)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid token: '${token}'`);
  }
  return parseInt(token, 10);
}

function validateField(
  fieldStr: string,
  bound: FieldBound
): void {
  if (!fieldStr || fieldStr.trim() === '') {
    throw new Error(`Field '${bound.name}' cannot be empty`);
  }

  const items = fieldStr.split(',');
  for (const item of items) {
    if (!item) {
      throw new Error(`Empty element in field '${bound.name}'`);
    }

    let base = item;
    if (item.includes('/')) {
      const parts = item.split('/');
      if (parts.length !== 2) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid step expression '${item}' in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      base = parts[0];
      const stepStr = parts[1];
      if (!/^\d+$/.test(stepStr)) {
        throw new Error(`Step '${stepStr}' must be a positive integer in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      const step = parseInt(stepStr, 10);
      if (step < 1) {
        throw new Error(`Step must be >= 1 in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      if (base === '*') {
        continue;
      }
    }

    if (base === '*') {
      continue;
    }

    if (base.includes('-')) {
      const rangeParts = base.split('-');
      if (rangeParts.length !== 2) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid range expression '${base}' in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      const start = parseToken(rangeParts[0], bound.nameMap);
      const end = parseToken(rangeParts[1], bound.nameMap);
      if (start < bound.min || start > bound.max || end < bound.min || end > bound.max) {
        throw new Error(`Range values '${base}' must be between ${bound.min} and ${bound.max} in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
      if (start > end) {
        throw new Error(`Range start '${start}' must be <= end '${end}' in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
    } else {
      const val = parseToken(base, bound.nameMap);
      if (val < bound.min || val > bound.max) {
        throw new Error(`Value '${val}' out of range [${bound.min}, ${bound.max}] in field '${bound.name}'`);
      }
    }
  }
}

export function cronValidator(): ValidatorFn {
  return (control: AbstractControl): ValidationErrors | null => {
    const value = control.value;
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {
      return null;
    }

    if (typeof value !== 'string') {
      return { invalidCron: { message: 'Cron expression must be a string' } };
    }

    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (trimmed === '') {
      return null;
    }

    const fields = trimmed.split(/\s+/);
    if (fields.length !== 5) {
      return {
        invalidCron: {
          message: `Expected exactly 5 fields (<minute> <hour> <day of month> <month> <day of week>), got ${fields.length}`,
        },
      };
    }

    try {
      for (let i = 0; i < FIELD_BOUNDS.length; i++) {
        validateField(fields[i], FIELD_BOUNDS[i]);
      }
      return null;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const errorMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Invalid cron syntax';
      return {
        invalidCron: {
          message: errorMsg,
        },
      };
    }
  };
}

export function jsonObjectValidator(): ValidatorFn {
  return (control: AbstractControl): ValidationErrors | null => {
    const val = control.value;
    if (!val || (typeof val === 'string' && !val.trim())) {
      return null;
    }
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(val);
      if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        return { invalidJson: 'ظˆط±ظˆط¯غŒ ط¨ط§غŒط¯ غŒع© ط´غŒط، ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± JSON ط¨ط§ط´ط¯ (ظ…ط§ظ†ظ†ط¯: {"key": "value"})' };

    }
  };
}

              @if (form.controls.cron_expression.errors?.['invalidCron']) {
                <div class="cron-error-feedback">
                  <i class="pi pi-times-circle"></i>
                  <span>{{ form.controls.cron_expression.errors?.['invalidCron']?.message }}</span>
                </div>
              } @else if (form.controls.cron_expression.value && form.controls.cron_expression.valid) {
                <div class="cron-valid-feedback">
                  <div class="cron-valid-line">
                    <i class="pi pi-check-circle"></i>
                    <span>ط§ظ„ع¯ظˆغŒ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ (Valid 5-part cron schedule)</span>

                  <div class="cron-human-summary">{{ getCronHumanSummary(form.controls.cron_expression.value) }}</div>

              }
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Collapsible/Optional JSONB Custom Params -->
          <div class="form-row">
            <div class="form-group col-span-2 custom-params-container">

                type="button"

                (click)="isCustomParamsOpen.update(v => !v)"
                [attr.aria-expanded]="isCustomParamsOpen()"

                <div class="toggle-title-group">
                  <i class="pi" [ngClass]="isCustomParamsOpen() ? 'pi-chevron-down' : 'pi-chevron-left'"></i>
                  <i class="pi pi-cog"></i>
                  <span class="custom-params-title">طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ط§ط¶ط§ظپغŒ ط§طھطµط§ظ„ (Custom Params JSONB)</span>

                      ظپط±ظ…طھ JSON ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯. ظ¾غŒط´â€Œظپط±ط¶: <code>&#123;&#125;</code>

export class ConnectionDialogComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);
  private readonly connectionService = inject(ConnectionService);

  @Input() connectionToEdit: DatabaseConnection | null = null;
  @Input() preselectedCategoryId: string | null = null;

  @Output() readonly save = new EventEmitter<ConnectionCreateRequest>();
  @Output() readonly cancel = new EventEmitter<void>();

  readonly supportedEngines: DatabaseEngine[] = [
    'POSTGRESQL',
    'MSSQL',
    'ORACLE',
    'MYSQL',
    'CLICKHOUSE',
    'MONGODB',
  ];

  private readonly defaultPorts: Record<DatabaseEngine, number> = {
    POSTGRESQL: 5432,
    MSSQL: 1433,
    ORACLE: 1521,
    MYSQL: 3306,
    CLICKHOUSE: 8123,
    MONGODB: 27017,
  };

  readonly isSubmitting = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly isCustomParamsOpen = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Category Tree Signals
  readonly rawCategories = signal<CategoryTreeNode[]>([]);
  readonly isLoadingCategories = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly categorySearchTerm = signal<string>('');
  readonly expandedNodeIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set<string>());
  readonly selectedCategoryName = signal<string | null>(null);

  readonly form = this.fb.group({
    name: this.fb.control<string>('', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1), Validators.maxLength(200)],
    }),
    category_id: this.fb.control<string | null>(null),
    engine_type: this.fb.control<DatabaseEngine>('POSTGRESQL', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required],
    }),
    host: this.fb.control<string>('localhost', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1), Validators.maxLength(255)],
    }),
    port: this.fb.control<number>(5432, {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required, Validators.min(1), Validators.max(65535)],
    }),
    database_name: this.fb.control<string>('', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1), Validators.maxLength(255)],
    }),
    default_schema: this.fb.control<string | null>('public'),
    owner: this.fb.control<string>('admin', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required, Validators.maxLength(255)],
    }),
    username: this.fb.control<string>('', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required],
    }),
    password: this.fb.control<string>('', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [Validators.required],
    }),
    cron_expression: this.fb.control<string | null>('', {
      validators: [cronValidator()],
    }),
    custom_params_json: this.fb.control<string>('{}', {
      nonNullable: true,
      validators: [jsonObjectValidator()],
    }),
  });

  // Flattened and filtered category list for display in the tree picker
  readonly flattenedCategoryList = computed<FlattenedCategoryNode[]>(() => {
    const raw = this.rawCategories();
    const expanded = this.expandedNodeIds();
    const term = this.categorySearchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();

    const result: FlattenedCategoryNode[] = [];

    const traverse = (nodes: CategoryTreeNode[], level: number, parentPath: string) => {
      for (const node of nodes) {
        const isLeaf = !node.children || node.children.length === 0;
        const currentPath = parentPath ? `${parentPath} > ${node.name}` : node.name;

        // If searching, show matching leaves or parents that contain matching children
        if (term) {
          const matchesSelf = node.name.toLowerCase().includes(term);
          const hasMatchingChild = this.hasMatchingDescendant(node, term);
          if (matchesSelf || hasMatchingChild) {
            result.push({ node, level, isLeaf, parentPath: currentPath });
            if (node.children && node.children.length > 0) {
              traverse(node.children, level + 1, currentPath);
            }
          }
        } else {
          result.push({ node, level, isLeaf, parentPath: currentPath });
          if (!isLeaf && expanded.has(node.id) && node.children) {
            traverse(node.children, level + 1, currentPath);
          }
        }
      }
    };

    traverse(raw, 0, '');
    return result;
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadCategoryTree();

    if (this.connectionToEdit) {
      const conn = this.connectionToEdit;
      const customParamsVal =
        conn.custom_params && Object.keys(conn.custom_params).length > 0
          ? JSON.stringify(conn.custom_params, null, 2)
          : '{}';
      this.form.patchValue({
        name: conn.name,
        category_id: conn.category_id || null,
        engine_type: conn.engine_type,
        host: conn.host,
        port: conn.port,
        database_name: conn.database_name,
        default_schema: conn.default_schema || '',
        owner: conn.owner,
        username: conn.masked_credentials?.['username'] || 'admin',
        password: '',
        cron_expression: conn.sync_schedule?.cron_expression || '',
        custom_params_json: customParamsVal,
      });
      if (conn.category_name) {
        this.selectedCategoryName.set(conn.category_name);
      }
      if (conn.custom_params && Object.keys(conn.custom_params).length > 0) {
        this.isCustomParamsOpen.set(true);
      }
      // In edit mode, password is optional
      this.form.controls.password.clearValidators();
      this.form.controls.password.updateValueAndValidity();
    } else if (this.preselectedCategoryId) {
      this.form.controls.category_id.setValue(this.preselectedCategoryId);
    }
  }

  loadCategoryTree(): void {
    this.isLoadingCategories.set(true);
    this.connectionService.getCategoryTree().subscribe({
      next: (tree) => {
        this.rawCategories.set(tree);
        this.isLoadingCategories.set(false);

        // Auto-expand parents if category is already selected
        const currentCatId = this.form.controls.category_id.value;
        if (currentCatId) {
          const found = this.findNodeAndAncestors(tree, currentCatId);
          if (found) {
            this.selectedCategoryName.set(found.target.name);
            this.expandedNodeIds.update((prev) => {
              const next = new Set(prev);
              found.ancestors.forEach((aId) => next.add(aId));
              return next;
            });
          }
        } else {
          // Expand first level by default for convenience
          const initialSet = new Set<string>();
          tree.forEach((root) => initialSet.add(root.id));
          this.expandedNodeIds.set(initialSet);
        }
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingCategories.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  private hasMatchingDescendant(node: CategoryTreeNode, term: string): boolean {
    if (!node.children || node.children.length === 0) return false;
    for (const child of node.children) {
      if (child.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) || this.hasMatchingDescendant(child, term)) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  private findNodeAndAncestors(
    nodes: CategoryTreeNode[],
    targetId: string,
    currentPath: string[] = []
  ): { target: CategoryTreeNode; ancestors: string[] } | null {
    for (const node of nodes) {
      if (node.id === targetId) {
        return { target: node, ancestors: currentPath };
      }
      if (node.children && node.children.length > 0) {
        const found = this.findNodeAndAncestors(node.children, targetId, [...currentPath, node.id]);
        if (found) return found;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  onSearchInput(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    this.categorySearchTerm.set(input.value);
  }

  toggleExpanded(nodeId: string): void {
    this.expandedNodeIds.update((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(nodeId)) {
        next.delete(nodeId);
      } else {
        next.add(nodeId);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  onNodeClick(item: FlattenedCategoryNode): void {
    if (!item.isLeaf) {
      // Parent node: toggle expand/collapse, cannot select
      this.toggleExpanded(item.node.id);
      return;
    }
    // Leaf node: select this leaf!
    this.form.controls.category_id.setValue(item.node.id);
    this.selectedCategoryName.set(item.node.name);
  }

  clearCategorySelection(): void {
    this.form.controls.category_id.setValue(null);
    this.selectedCategoryName.set(null);
  }

  onEngineChange(): void {
    const engine = this.form.controls.engine_type.value;
    if (engine && this.defaultPorts[engine]) {
      this.form.controls.port.setValue(this.defaultPorts[engine]);
    }
  }

  onBackdropClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if ((event.target as HTMLElement).classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
      this.cancel.emit();
    }
  }

  getCronHumanSummary(cron: string | null): string {
    if (!cron) return '';
    const trimmed = cron.trim();
    if (trimmed === '0 2 * * *') return 'ظ‡ط± ط´ط¨ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ°غ²:غ°غ° ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯ (Daily at 02:00 AM)';
    if (trimmed === '0 0 * * *') return 'ظ‡ط± ط±ظˆط² ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ±غ²:غ°غ° ط´ط¨ (Daily at midnight)';
    if (trimmed === '0 4 * * *') return 'ظ‡ط± ط´ط¨ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ°غ´:غ°غ° ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯ (Daily at 04:00 AM)';
    if (trimmed === '0 * * * *') return 'ظ‡ط± غŒع© ط³ط§ط¹طھ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± (Every hour)';
    if (trimmed === '*/30 * * * *') return 'ظ‡ط± غ³غ° ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± (Every 30 minutes)';
    if (trimmed === '*/15 * * * *') return 'ظ‡ط± غ±غµ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± (Every 15 minutes)';
    const parts = trimmed.split(/\s+/);
    if (parts.length === 5) {
      const [m, h] = parts;
      if (!isNaN(Number(m)) && !isNaN(Number(h))) {
        return `ظ‡ط± ط±ظˆط² ط³ط§ط¹طھ ${h.padStart(2, '0')}:${m.padStart(2, '0')} (Daily at ${h}:${m})`;
      }
    }
    return 'ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط´ط¯ظ‡ (Custom Scheduled Sync)';
  }

  onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      if (this.form.controls.custom_params_json.invalid) {
        this.isCustomParamsOpen.set(true);
      }
      return;
    }

    const val = this.form.getRawValue();
    let customParams: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    const rawCustom = val.custom_params_json?.trim();
    if (rawCustom) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(rawCustom);
        if (typeof parsed === 'object' && parsed !== null && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
          customParams = parsed;
        } else {
          this.form.controls.custom_params_json.setErrors({ invalidJson: 'ظˆط±ظˆط¯غŒ ط¨ط§غŒط¯ غŒع© ط´غŒط، ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± JSON ط¨ط§ط´ط¯' });
          this.isCustomParamsOpen.set(true);
          return;
        }
      } catch {
        this.form.controls.custom_params_json.setErrors({ invalidJson: 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± JSON: ظپط±ظ…طھ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط§ط³طھ' });
        this.isCustomParamsOpen.set(true);
        return;
      }
    }

    const creds: Record<string, unknown> = {
      username: val.username,
    };
    if (val.password && val.password.trim()) {
      creds['password'] = val.password;
    }

    const request: ConnectionCreateRequest = {
      name: val.name,
      engine_type: val.engine_type,
      host: val.host,
      port: val.port,
      database_name: val.database_name,
      default_schema: val.default_schema?.trim() || undefined,
      owner: val.owner,
      category_id: val.category_id || undefined,
      credentials: creds,
      custom_params: customParams,
      cron_expression: val.cron_expression?.trim() || undefined,
    };

    this.save.emit(request);
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ConnectionService } from './services/connection.service';
import { ConnectionDialogComponent } from './connection-dialog.component';
import { HasPermissionDirective } from '../../core/directives/has-permission.directive';
import {
  ConnectionCreateRequest,
  ConnectionTestResult,
  DatabaseConnection,
} from './models/connection.models';
import { CategoryTreeNode } from '../catalog/models/catalog.models';

export interface CategoryTreeItem {
  node: CategoryTreeNode;
  level: number;
  isLeaf: boolean;
  dbCount: number;
}

export type ConnectionSortKey = 'name' | 'host' | 'database_name' | 'owner';
export type SortDirection = 'asc' | 'desc';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-connection-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, ConnectionDialogComponent, HasPermissionDirective],
  templateUrl: './connection-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./connection-list.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class ConnectionListComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly connectionService = inject(ConnectionService);

  readonly connections = signal<DatabaseConnection[]>([]);
  readonly categoryTree = signal<CategoryTreeNode[]>([]);
  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isLoadingTree = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly showDialog = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly connectionToEdit = signal<DatabaseConnection | null>(null);
  readonly preselectedCategoryId = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly testingConnectionId = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly testResults = signal<Record<string, ConnectionTestResult>>({});
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Delete confirmation
  readonly connectionToDelete = signal<DatabaseConnection | null>(null);

  // Category Tree Navigation Signals
  readonly selectedCategory = signal<CategoryTreeNode | null>(null);
  readonly expandedCategoryIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set<string>());
  readonly categorySearch = signal<string>('');

  // Table state: filters, sorting, pagination, expanded rows
  readonly searchTerm = signal<string>('');
  readonly engineFilter = signal<string>('ALL');
  readonly statusFilter = signal<'ALL' | 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE'>('ALL');
  readonly sortKey = signal<ConnectionSortKey | null>(null);
  readonly sortDirection = signal<SortDirection>('asc');
  readonly currentPage = signal<number>(1);
  readonly pageSize = signal<number>(10);
  readonly expandedRowIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set<string>());
  readonly availablePageSizes: number[] = [10, 20, 50];

  // Map of category_id -> count of databases assigned
  readonly categoryDatabaseCounts = computed<Record<string, number>>(() => {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const c of this.connections()) {
      if (c.category_id) {
        counts[c.category_id] = (counts[c.category_id] || 0) + 1;
      }
    }
    return counts;
  });

  // Flattened hierarchical tree items with indent levels and leaf detection
  readonly flattenedCategoryTree = computed<CategoryTreeItem[]>(() => {
    const raw = this.categoryTree();
    const expanded = this.expandedCategoryIds();
    const counts = this.categoryDatabaseCounts();
    const term = this.categorySearch().trim().toLowerCase();

    const items: CategoryTreeItem[] = [];

    const traverse = (nodes: CategoryTreeNode[], level: number) => {
      for (const node of nodes) {
        const isLeaf = !node.children || node.children.length === 0;
        const dbCount = counts[node.id] || 0;

        if (term) {
          const matchesSelf = node.name.toLowerCase().includes(term);
          const hasMatchingDescendant = this.hasMatchingDescendant(node, term);
          if (matchesSelf || hasMatchingDescendant) {
            items.push({ node, level, isLeaf, dbCount });
            if (node.children && node.children.length > 0) {
              traverse(node.children, level + 1);
            }
          }
        } else {
          items.push({ node, level, isLeaf, dbCount });
          if (!isLeaf && expanded.has(node.id) && node.children) {
            traverse(node.children, level + 1);
          }
        }
      }
    };

    traverse(raw, 0);
    return items;
  });

  // Helper to collect a category ID and all its descendant category IDs
  private getDescendantCategoryIds(node: CategoryTreeNode): Set<string> {
    const ids = new Set<string>();
    const collect = (curr: CategoryTreeNode) => {
      ids.add(curr.id);
      if (curr.children && curr.children.length > 0) {
        for (const child of curr.children) {
          collect(child);
        }
      }
    };
    collect(node);
    return ids;
  }

  // Connections of the selected category (parent branch with all descendants, or leaf) or all
  readonly filteredConnections = computed<DatabaseConnection[]>(() => {
    const selected = this.selectedCategory();
    const all = this.connections();
    if (!selected) {
      return all;
    }
    const allowedCategoryIds = this.getDescendantCategoryIds(selected);
    return all.filter((c) => !!c.category_id && allowedCategoryIds.has(c.category_id));
  });

  // Engines present in the current scope (for the engine filter dropdown)
  readonly availableEngines = computed<string[]>(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    for (const c of this.filteredConnections()) {
      set.add(c.engine_type);
    }
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  });

  // Search + engine + status filters, then sorting
  readonly tableConnections = computed<DatabaseConnection[]>(() => {
    const term = this.searchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    const engine = this.engineFilter();
    const status = this.statusFilter();
    const key = this.sortKey();
    const dir = this.sortDirection();

    let list = this.filteredConnections().filter((c) => {
      if (engine !== 'ALL' && c.engine_type !== engine) return false;
      if (status === 'ACTIVE' && !c.is_active) return false;
      if (status === 'INACTIVE' && c.is_active) return false;
      if (term) {
        const haystack = [c.name, c.host, c.database_name, c.owner, c.category_name, c.default_schema]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(' ')
          .toLowerCase();
        if (!haystack.includes(term)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });

    if (key) {
      const factor = dir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
      list = [...list].sort((a, b) => {
        const av = String(a[key] ?? '');
        const bv = String(b[key] ?? '');
        return av.localeCompare(bv, 'fa') * factor;
      });
    }
    return list;
  });

  readonly totalPages = computed<number>(() =>
    Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.tableConnections().length / this.pageSize()))
  );

  // Current page clamped to a valid range (e.g. after deleting the last row of a page)
  readonly safePage = computed<number>(() => Math.min(this.currentPage(), this.totalPages()));

  readonly paginatedConnections = computed<DatabaseConnection[]>(() => {
    const start = (this.safePage() - 1) * this.pageSize();
    return this.tableConnections().slice(start, start + this.pageSize());
  });

  readonly pageDisplayStart = computed<number>(() =>
    this.tableConnections().length === 0 ? 0 : (this.safePage() - 1) * this.pageSize() + 1
  );

  readonly pageDisplayEnd = computed<number>(() =>
    Math.min(this.safePage() * this.pageSize(), this.tableConnections().length)
  );

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadConnections();
    this.loadCategoryTree();
  }

  loadConnections(): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.connectionService.getConnections().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.connections.set(items);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ظ„غŒط³طھ ط§طھطµط§ظ„ط§طھ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡';
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  loadCategoryTree(): void {
    this.isLoadingTree.set(true);
    this.connectionService.getCategoryTree().subscribe({
      next: (tree) => {
        this.categoryTree.set(tree);
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);

        // Expand root categories by default
        const initialExpanded = new Set<string>();
        tree.forEach((t) => initialExpanded.add(t.id));
        this.expandedCategoryIds.set(initialExpanded);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  private hasMatchingDescendant(node: CategoryTreeNode, term: string): boolean {
    if (!node.children || node.children.length === 0) return false;
    for (const child of node.children) {
      if (child.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) || this.hasMatchingDescendant(child, term)) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Category tree â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  onCategorySearchInput(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    this.categorySearch.set(input.value);
  }

  toggleExpandCategory(categoryId: string): void {
    this.expandedCategoryIds.update((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(categoryId)) {
        next.delete(categoryId);
      } else {
        next.add(categoryId);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  selectCategory(node: CategoryTreeNode | null): void {
    if (!node) {
      this.selectedCategory.set(null);
      this.resetTableView();
      return;
    }
    this.selectedCategory.set(node);
    this.resetTableView();
  }

  // Reset engine filter (it may not exist in the new scope), page and open rows
  private resetTableView(): void {
    this.engineFilter.set('ALL');
    this.currentPage.set(1);
    this.expandedRowIds.set(new Set<string>());
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Table: filters, sort, pagination, rows â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  onFilterChanged(): void {
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  toggleSort(key: ConnectionSortKey): void {
    if (this.sortKey() === key) {
      if (this.sortDirection() === 'asc') {
        this.sortDirection.set('desc');
      } else {
        // Third click clears the sort
        this.sortKey.set(null);
        this.sortDirection.set('asc');
      }
    } else {
      this.sortKey.set(key);
      this.sortDirection.set('asc');
    }
  }

  sortIcon(key: ConnectionSortKey): string {
    if (this.sortKey() !== key) return 'pi-sort-alt';
    return this.sortDirection() === 'asc' ? 'pi-sort-amount-up-alt' : 'pi-sort-amount-down';
  }

  goToPage(page: number): void {
    const target = Math.min(Math.max(1, page), this.totalPages());
    this.currentPage.set(target);
  }

  changePageSize(size: number | string): void {
    this.pageSize.set(Number(size));
    this.currentPage.set(1);
  }

  toggleRow(id: string): void {
    this.expandedRowIds.update((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) {
        next.delete(id);
      } else {
        next.add(id);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Dialog â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  openCreateDialog(categoryId?: string): void {
    this.connectionToEdit.set(null);
    this.preselectedCategoryId.set(categoryId || this.selectedCategory()?.id || null);
    this.showDialog.set(true);
  }

  openEditDialog(conn: DatabaseConnection): void {
    this.connectionToEdit.set(conn);
    this.preselectedCategoryId.set(null);
    this.showDialog.set(true);
  }

  closeDialog(): void {
    this.showDialog.set(false);
    this.connectionToEdit.set(null);
    this.preselectedCategoryId.set(null);
  }

  onSaveConnection(request: ConnectionCreateRequest): void {
    const editConn = this.connectionToEdit();
    if (editConn) {
      this.connectionService.updateConnection(editConn.id, request).subscribe({
        next: (updatedConn) => {
          this.connections.update((list) =>
            list.map((c) => (c.id === updatedConn.id ? updatedConn : c))
          );
          this.closeDialog();
          this.loadCategoryTree();
        },
        error: (err: unknown) => {
          const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط±ظˆط²ط±ط³ط§ظ†غŒ ط§طھطµط§ظ„ ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³';
          this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        },
      });
    } else {
      this.connectionService.createConnection(request).subscribe({
        next: (newConn) => {
          this.connections.update((list) => [newConn, ...list]);
          this.closeDialog();
          this.loadCategoryTree();
        },
        error: (err: unknown) => {
          const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط«ط¨طھ ط§طھطµط§ظ„ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡';
          this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        },
      });
    }
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Delete (in-app confirmation instead of window.confirm) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  requestDelete(conn: DatabaseConnection): void {
    this.connectionToDelete.set(conn);
  }

  cancelDelete(): void {
    this.connectionToDelete.set(null);
  }

  confirmDelete(): void {
    const target = this.connectionToDelete();
    if (!target) return;

    this.connectionService.deleteConnection(target.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.connections.update((list) => list.filter((c) => c.id !== target.id));
        this.connectionToDelete.set(null);
        this.loadCategoryTree();
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط­ط°ظپ ط§طھطµط§ظ„ ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³';
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.connectionToDelete.set(null);
      },
    });
  }

  // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Test connection â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  onTestConnection(id: string): void {
    this.testingConnectionId.set(id);

    this.connectionService.testConnection(id).subscribe({
      next: (result) => {
        this.testResults.update((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          [id]: result,
        }));
        this.testingConnectionId.set(null);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const errorResult: ConnectionTestResult = {
          success: false,
          latency_ms: 5000.0,
          dialect: 'POSTGRESQL',
          message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Connection test failed',
          tested_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
        this.testResults.update((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          [id]: errorResult,
        }));
        this.testingConnectionId.set(null);
      },
    });
  }
}

  total_tables: number;
  total_columns: number;

}

import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  CompletenessReport,
  ClassificationReport,
  ReportRefreshResponse,
} from '../models/report.models';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class ReportService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/reports';

  /**
   * Retrieves aggregated metadata completeness metrics across all or selected databases.
   */
  getCompletenessReport(connectionIds?: string[]): Observable<CompletenessReport> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (connectionIds && connectionIds.length > 0) {
      connectionIds.forEach((id) => {
        params = params.append('connection_ids', id);
      });
    }
    return this.http.get<CompletenessReport>(`${this.baseUrl}/completeness`, { params });
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves security classification and PII distribution metrics across all or selected databases.
   */
  getClassificationDistribution(connectionIds?: string[]): Observable<ClassificationReport> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (connectionIds && connectionIds.length > 0) {
      connectionIds.forEach((id) => {
        params = params.append('connection_ids', id);
      });
    }
    return this.http.get<ClassificationReport>(`${this.baseUrl}/classification-distribution`, { params });
  }

  /**
   * Triggers non-blocking concurrent refresh of all reporting materialized views.
   */
  refreshMaterializedViews(): Observable<ReportRefreshResponse> {
    return this.http.post<ReportRefreshResponse>(`${this.baseUrl}/refresh`, {});
  }

  /**
   * Exports executive reporting summary to JSON or CSV format.
   */
  exportReports(format: 'json' | 'csv', connectionIds?: string[]): Observable<Blob> {
    let params = new HttpParams().set('format', format);
    if (connectionIds && connectionIds.length > 0) {
      connectionIds.forEach((id) => {
        params = params.append('connection_ids', id);
      });
    }
    return this.http.get(`${this.baseUrl}/export`, {
      params,
      responseType: 'blob',
    });
  }
}

  ],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,

export class ClassificationSunburstComponent {
  readonly report = input<ClassificationReport | null>(null);
  readonly title = input<string>('طھظˆط²غŒط¹ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…ط§ظ†ع¯غŒ ظˆ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط­ط³ط§ط³ (Classification & PII Distribution)');

  readonly chartOptions = computed<echarts.EChartsCoreOption>(() => {
    const rep = this.report();
    const items = rep?.breakdown ?? [];

    const colorMap: Record<string, string> = {
      PUBLIC: '#10b981',
      INTERNAL: '#3b82f6',
      CONFIDENTIAL: '#f59e0b',
      RESTRICTED: '#ef4444',
    };

    const sunburstData = items.map((item) => ({
      name: item.classification_level,
      value: item.column_count,
      itemStyle: {
        color: colorMap[item.classification_level.toUpperCase()] ?? '#8b5cf6',
      },
      children: [
        {
          name: `${item.affected_table_count} Tables`,
          value: item.column_count,
          itemStyle: {
            color: colorMap[item.classification_level.toUpperCase()] ?? '#8b5cf6',
            opacity: 0.75,
          },
        },
      ],
    }));

    return {
      tooltip: {
        trigger: 'item',
        formatter: (params: any) => {
          const val = params.value ? Number(params.value).toLocaleString() : '0';
          return `<strong>${params.name}</strong><br/>Columns: ${val}`;
        },
      },
      series: [
        {
          type: 'sunburst',
          data: sunburstData,
          radius: [0, '90%'],
          sort: undefined,
          emphasis: {
            focus: 'ancestor',
          },
          levels: [
            {},
            {
              r0: '15%',
              r: '55%',
              itemStyle: {
                borderWidth: 2,
                borderColor: '#ffffff',
              },
              label: {
                rotate: 'tangential',
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#ffffff',
              },
            },
            {
              r0: '55%',
              r: '88%',
              label: {
                align: 'right',
                fontSize: 11,
                color: '#1e293b',
              },
              itemStyle: {
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: '#ffffff',
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };
  });
}

@Component({

=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\reports\executive-dashboard.component.ts ===
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ReportService } from './services/report.service';
import { HasPermissionDirective } from '../../core/directives/has-permission.directive';
import {
  ClassificationReport,
  CompletenessReport,
  DatabaseCompletenessItem,
} from './models/report.models';
import { CompletenessGaugeComponent } from './completeness-gauge.component';
import { ClassificationSunburstComponent } from './classification-sunburst.component';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-executive-dashboard',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    CompletenessGaugeComponent,
    ClassificationSunburstComponent,
    HasPermissionDirective,
  ],
  templateUrl: './executive-dashboard.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./executive-dashboard.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class ExecutiveDashboardComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly reportService = inject(ReportService);

  readonly completenessReport = signal<CompletenessReport | null>(null);
  readonly classificationReport = signal<ClassificationReport | null>(null);
  readonly isLoading = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isRefreshing = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isExporting = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly refreshSuccessMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  // Available databases for filtering
  readonly availableDatabases = signal<{ id: string; name: string; engine_type: string }[]>([]);
  readonly selectedConnectionIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Computed metrics for executive KPI cards
  readonly overallScore = computed<number>(
    () => this.completenessReport()?.overall_completeness_pct ?? 0
  );
  readonly totalDatabases = computed<number>(
    () => this.completenessReport()?.total_databases ?? 0
  );
  readonly totalTables = computed<number>(
    () => this.completenessReport()?.total_tables ?? 0
  );
  readonly totalColumns = computed<number>(
    () => this.completenessReport()?.total_columns ?? 0
  );
  readonly totalClassified = computed<number>(
    () => this.classificationReport()?.total_classified_columns ?? 0
  );
  readonly databaseBreakdown = computed<DatabaseCompletenessItem[]>(
    () => this.completenessReport()?.database_breakdown ?? []
  );

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadDatabases();
    this.loadReports();
  }

  loadDatabases(): void {
    this.http.get<any[]>('/api/v1/connections').subscribe({
      next: (conns) => {
        this.availableDatabases.set(
          (conns || []).map((c) => ({ id: c.id, name: c.name, engine_type: c.engine_type }))
        );
      },
      error: () => {},
    });
  }

  toggleDatabaseFilter(dbId: string): void {
    const current = new Set(this.selectedConnectionIds());
    if (current.has(dbId)) {
      current.delete(dbId);
    } else {
      current.add(dbId);
    }
    this.selectedConnectionIds.set(current);
    this.loadReports();
  }

  clearDatabaseFilter(): void {
    if (this.selectedConnectionIds().size > 0) {
      this.selectedConnectionIds.set(new Set());
      this.loadReports();
    }
  }

  loadReports(): void {
    this.isLoading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    const ids = Array.from(this.selectedConnectionIds());

    forkJoin({
      completeness: this.reportService.getCompletenessReport(ids),
      classification: this.reportService.getClassificationDistribution(ids),
    }).subscribe({
      next: ({ completeness, classification }) => {
        this.completenessReport.set(completeness);
        this.classificationReport.set(classification);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg =
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ع¯ط²ط§ط±ط´ط§طھ ط­ط§ع©ظ…غŒطھ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡';
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  refreshViews(): void {
    this.isRefreshing.set(true);
    this.refreshSuccessMessage.set(null);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.reportService.refreshMaterializedViews().subscribe({
      next: (response) => {
        this.refreshSuccessMessage.set(response.message);
        this.isRefreshing.set(false);
        this.loadReports();
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg =
          err instanceof Error
            ? err.message
            : 'Failed to refresh materialized views concurrently';
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.isRefreshing.set(false);
      },
    });
  }

  exportReport(format: 'json' | 'csv'): void {
    this.isExporting.set(true);
    const ids = Array.from(this.selectedConnectionIds());
    this.reportService.exportReports(format, ids).subscribe({
      next: (blob) => {
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = `datacatalog_report_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.${format}`;
        a.click();
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        this.isExporting.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: unknown) => {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¯ط±غŒط§ظپطھ ط®ط±ظˆط¬غŒ ع¯ط²ط§ط±ط´';
        this.errorMessage.set(msg);
        this.isExporting.set(false);
      },
    });
  }
}

import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnInit,
  computed,
  inject,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule, ReactiveFormsModule, FormBuilder, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { cronValidator } from '../connections/validators/cron.validator';

export interface DatabaseNodeItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  engine_type: string;
  database_name: string;
  category_name?: string;
}

export interface CategoryHierarchyNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  databases: DatabaseNodeItem[];
  children: CategoryHierarchyNode[];
}

export interface SyncScheduleData {
  id: string;
  connection_id: string;
  cron_expression: string;
  is_enabled: boolean;
  last_run_at?: string | null;
  last_run_status?: string | null;
  last_run_message?: string | null;
  next_run_at?: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface CronPreset {
  label: string;
  expression: string;
  description: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-schedule-management',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, ReactiveFormsModule],
  templateUrl: './schedule-management.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./schedule-management.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class ScheduleManagementComponent implements OnInit {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);

  // States
  readonly isLoadingTree = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isLoadingSchedule = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isSavingSchedule = signal<boolean>(false);
  readonly isTriggeringSync = signal<boolean>(false);

  // Tree data
  readonly rawCategoryTree = signal<CategoryHierarchyNode[]>([]);
  readonly treeSearchTerm = signal<string>('');
  readonly expandedCategoryIds = signal<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Filtered Tree Computed
  readonly filteredTree = computed(() => {
    const term = this.treeSearchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    const tree = this.rawCategoryTree();
    if (!term) return tree;

    const filterNode = (node: CategoryHierarchyNode): CategoryHierarchyNode | null => {
      const nodeMatches = node.name.toLowerCase().includes(term);
      const filteredDbs = (node.databases || []).filter(
        (db) =>
          db.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
          db.database_name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
          db.engine_type.toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
      const filteredChildren = (node.children || [])
        .map((child) => filterNode(child))
        .filter((child): child is CategoryHierarchyNode => child !== null);

      if (nodeMatches || filteredDbs.length > 0 || filteredChildren.length > 0) {
        return {
          ...node,
          databases: nodeMatches ? node.databases : filteredDbs,
          children: filteredChildren,
        };
      }
      return null;
    };

    return tree.map((n) => filterNode(n)).filter((n): n is CategoryHierarchyNode => n !== null);
  });

  // Selected Database & Schedule
  readonly selectedDatabase = signal<DatabaseNodeItem | null>(null);
  readonly currentSchedule = signal<SyncScheduleData | null>(null);

  // Feedback Toasts
  readonly toastMessage = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly toastType = signal<'success' | 'error'>('success');
  private toastTimer: any = null;

  // Cron Presets
  readonly cronPresets: CronPreset[] = [
    { label: 'ظ‡ط± غ¶ ط³ط§ط¹طھ', expression: '0 */6 * * *', description: 'ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± غ¶ ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط¯ط± ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ طµظپط±' },
    { label: 'ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡ (غ² ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯)', expression: '0 2 * * *', description: 'ظ‡ط± ط´ط¨ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ²:غ°غ° ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯' },
    { label: 'ظ‡ظپطھع¯غŒ (ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§)', expression: '0 1 * * 5', description: 'ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ±:غ°غ° ط¨ط§ظ…ط¯ط§ط¯' },
    { label: 'ظ…ط§ظ‡ط§ظ†ظ‡ (ط±ظˆط² ط§ظˆظ„)', expression: '0 0 1 * *', description: 'ط§ط¨طھط¯ط§غŒ ظ‡ط± ظ…ط§ظ‡ طھظ‚ظˆغŒظ…غŒ ط³ط§ط¹طھ غ°غ°:غ°غ°' },
    { label: 'ظ‡ط± غ±غµ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡', expression: '*/15 * * * *', description: 'ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± غ±غµ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± (ظ¾ط§غŒط´ ظ…ط¯ط§ظˆظ…)' },
  ];

  // Form
  readonly scheduleForm = this.fb.group({
    cron_expression: ['', [Validators.required, cronValidator()]],
    is_enabled: [true],
  });

  // Human readable live Persian preview of cron
  readonly cronHumanPreview = computed<string>(() => {
    const expr = this.scheduleForm.controls.cron_expression.value?.trim();
    if (!expr) return 'ط¹ط¨ط§ط±طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ظ†ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯.';

    const matched = this.cronPresets.find((p) => p.expression === expr);
    if (matched) return matched.description;

    const parts = expr.split(/\s+/);
    if (parts.length !== 5) return 'ظپط±ظ…طھ ع©ط±ظˆظ† ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ط§ظ‹ ط§ط² غµ ط¨ط®ط´ ظ…ط¬ط²ط§ ط¨ط§ ظپط§طµظ„ظ‡ طھط´ع©غŒظ„ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.';

    const [min, hour, dom, month, dow] = parts;
    if (min === '0' && hour.startsWith('*/')) {
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± ${hour.replace('*/', '')} ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط¯ط± ط§ط¨طھط¯ط§غŒ ط³ط§ط¹طھ`;
    }
    if (min.startsWith('*/') && hour === '*' && dom === '*' && month === '*' && dow === '*') {
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± ${min.replace('*/', '')} ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± ط¯ط± طھظ…ط§ظ… ط³ط§ط¹ط§طھ`;
    }
    if (!isNaN(Number(min)) && !isNaN(Number(hour)) && dom === '*' && month === '*' && dow === '*') {
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ‡ط± ط±ظˆط² ط±ط§ط³ ط³ط§ط¹طھ ${hour.padStart(2, '0')}:${min.padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
    if (!isNaN(Number(min)) && !isNaN(Number(hour)) && dom === '*' && month === '*' && !isNaN(Number(dow))) {
      const days = ['غŒع©â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ع†ظ‡ط§ط±ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ظ¾ظ†ط¬â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡', 'ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡', 'ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡'];
      const dayName = days[Number(dow)] || `ط±ظˆط² ${dow}`;
      return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ط¯ط± ${dayName}â€Œظ‡ط§ ط±ط§ط³ ط³ط§ط¹طھ ${hour.padStart(2, '0')}:${min.padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }
    return `ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ط¨ط± ط§ط³ط§ط³ ط§ظ„ع¯ظˆغŒ ط§ط®طھطµط§طµغŒ: ${expr}`;
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadCategoryTree();
  }

  loadCategoryTree(): void {
    this.isLoadingTree.set(true);
    this.http.get<CategoryHierarchyNode[]>('/api/v1/categories/tree').subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.rawCategoryTree.set(data || []);
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);

        // Auto-expand root categories and select first available database
        const rootIds = new Set<string>();
        let firstDb: DatabaseNodeItem | null = null;

        const findFirst = (nodes: CategoryHierarchyNode[]) => {
          for (const n of nodes) {
            rootIds.add(n.id);
            if (!firstDb && n.databases && n.databases.length > 0) {
              firstDb = { ...n.databases[0], category_name: n.name };
            }
            if (n.children) {
              findFirst(n.children);
            }
          }
        };

        findFirst(data || []);
        this.expandedCategoryIds.set(rootIds);

        if (firstDb) {
          this.selectDatabase(firstDb);
        }
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingTree.set(false);
        this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¯ط±غŒط§ظپطھ ط³ط§ط®طھط§ط± ط¯ط±ط®طھغŒ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡', 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  toggleCategory(id: string): void {
    const current = new Set(this.expandedCategoryIds());
    if (current.has(id)) {
      current.delete(id);
    } else {
      current.add(id);
    }
    this.expandedCategoryIds.set(current);
  }

  selectDatabase(db: DatabaseNodeItem): void {
    this.selectedDatabase.set(db);
    this.isLoadingSchedule.set(true);
    this.http.get<SyncScheduleData>(`/api/v1/connections/${db.id}/schedule`).subscribe({
      next: (schedule) => {
        this.currentSchedule.set(schedule);
        this.scheduleForm.patchValue({
          cron_expression: schedule.cron_expression || '0 2 * * *',
          is_enabled: schedule.is_enabled ?? true,
        });
        this.isLoadingSchedule.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoadingSchedule.set(false);
        this.showToast('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط¬ط²ط¦غŒط§طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡', 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  applyPreset(preset: CronPreset): void {
    this.scheduleForm.patchValue({
      cron_expression: preset.expression,
    });
    this.scheduleForm.controls.cron_expression.markAsDirty();
  }

  saveSchedule(): void {
    const db = this.selectedDatabase();
    if (!db || this.scheduleForm.invalid) {
      this.scheduleForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.isSavingSchedule.set(true);
    const payload = {
      cron_expression: this.scheduleForm.controls.cron_expression.value?.trim(),
      is_enabled: this.scheduleForm.controls.is_enabled.value ?? true,
    };

    this.http.put<SyncScheduleData>(`/api/v1/connections/${db.id}/schedule`, payload).subscribe({
      next: (updated) => {
        this.currentSchedule.set(updated);
        this.isSavingSchedule.set(false);
        this.showToast('طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯.', 'success');
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isSavingSchedule.set(false);
        const msg = err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ';
        this.showToast(msg, 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  triggerSyncNow(): void {
    const db = this.selectedDatabase();
    if (!db) return;

    this.isTriggeringSync.set(true);
    this.http.post<SyncScheduleData>(`/api/v1/connections/${db.id}/sync`, {}).subscribe({
      next: (updated) => {
        this.currentSchedule.set(updated);
        this.isTriggeringSync.set(false);
        this.showToast(`ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ظ…طھط§ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ¾ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ آ«${db.name}آ» ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯.`, 'success');
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isTriggeringSync.set(false);
        const msg = err?.error?.detail || 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒ ظپط±ط¢غŒظ†ط¯ ظ‡ظ…ع¯ط§ظ…â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ';
        this.showToast(msg, 'error');
      },
    });
  }

  showToast(message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' = 'success'): void {
    if (this.toastTimer) clearTimeout(this.toastTimer);
    this.toastMessage.set(message);
    this.toastType.set(type);
    this.toastTimer = setTimeout(() => this.toastMessage.set(null), 4500);
  }

  closeToast(): void {
    this.toastMessage.set(null);
  }
}

=== FILE: D:\DataCatalog\frontend\src\app\features\taxonomy\services\category.service.ts ===
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { CategoryTreeNode } from '../../catalog/models/catalog.models';

export interface CategoryResponse {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
  assigned_tables_count: number;
}

export interface CategoryCreateRequest {
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  status?: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
}

export interface CategoryUpdateRequest {
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  parent_id?: string | null;
  status?: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE';
}

export interface CategoryAssignmentItem {
  id: string;
  category_id: string;
  category_name?: string;
  entity_type: string;
  entity_id: string;
  table_name?: string;
  schema_name?: string;
  database_name?: string;
  created_at?: string;
}

export interface AvailableTableItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  schema_name: string;
  database_name: string;
  row_count_estimate?: number;
}

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class CategoryService {
  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly baseUrl = '/api/v1/categories';

  getCategoryTree(): Observable<CategoryTreeNode[]> {
    return this.http.get<CategoryTreeNode[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/tree`);
  }

  listCategories(): Observable<CategoryResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<CategoryResponse[]>(this.baseUrl);
  }

  createCategory(payload: CategoryCreateRequest): Observable<CategoryResponse> {
    return this.http.post<CategoryResponse>(this.baseUrl, payload);
  }

  updateCategory(id: string, payload: CategoryUpdateRequest): Observable<CategoryResponse> {
    return this.http.put<CategoryResponse>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`, payload);
  }

  deleteCategory(id: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/${id}`);
  }

  getAssignments(categoryId?: string, entityType = 'TABLE'): Observable<CategoryAssignmentItem[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (categoryId) {
      params = params.set('category_id', categoryId);
    }
    if (entityType) {
      params = params.set('entity_type', entityType);
    }
    return this.http.get<CategoryAssignmentItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/assignments`, { params });
  }

  assignTable(categoryId: string, tableId: string): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post(`${this.baseUrl}/assignments`, {
      category_id: categoryId,
      entity_type: 'TABLE',
      entity_id: tableId,
    });
  }

  unassignTable(categoryId: string, tableId: string): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.baseUrl}/assignments/${categoryId}/TABLE/${tableId}`);
  }

  getAvailableTables(search?: string): Observable<AvailableTableItem[]> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (search && search.trim()) {
      params = params.set('search', search.trim());
    }
    return this.http.get<AvailableTableItem[]>(`${this.baseUrl}/available-tables`, { params });
  }
}
